2.0.0

Fixed

- **`deploy` port resolution no longer mutates the global `os.environ` ([560](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/560)).** `deploy._ports.resolve_port` previously honored an explicit `environ` override by save/clear/update/restoring the live process environment around the `load_serve_config` call — not thread-safe (a concurrent reader during the call saw a cleared env with the override swapped in). The mapping is now threaded straight through: `load_serve_config(agent_root, environ=...)` and `_parse_serve_md(text, environ=...)` accept an optional `environ` (defaulting to `os.environ`) and read overrides from it, so `resolve_port` passes the dict cleanly and the global env is never touched. Regression test snapshots the global env at loader-invocation time (the only point old/new behavior diverge) with a verified strip-RED negative control.

Added

- **`atomic-agents deploy` — the deployment conductor (issue [558](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/558) — spec/49 DRAFT, v1.5).** New `atomic_agents/deploy/` package: a conductor that takes an operator from "I have an agent folder" to "running, supervised, and verified on loopback," orchestrating the existing `init` / `doctor` / `serve` surfaces (it owns no new runtime). CLI: `atomic-agents deploy <agent>` (`--plan` dry-run, `--yes`, `--verify-call`, `--port`, `--agents-root`) plus `deploy status <agent>` and `deploy down <agent>`. Planner→executor with `auto`/`consent`/`manual` step tags; `--plan` is side-effect-free and unbilled (MUST 6). Supervision installs a user-level launchd agent (domain `gui/$UID`, no `sudo`) with an absolute `ProgramArguments` path and `EnvironmentVariables` injecting `HOME`/`USER`/`PATH`/`ATOMIC_AGENTS_ROOT` — the provider key is sourced from Keychain/keys.json and never written plaintext into the plist unless an env var is its only source (MUST 4/5); `deploy` never runs `serve` in-process. Non-mutating verify on loopback — `/healthz` `status=="ok"` AND doctor `overall_exit_code(results)==0` (a 200 alone never counts); a real billed `/call` is opt-in via `--verify-call` (MUST 9). Idempotent re-deploy (bootout→bootstrap; the pre-bootstrap bind-probe is skipped only when our own label is actually RUNNING, so a foreign port holder during a crashed/loaded re-deploy still fails loud — MUST 7/10); rollback (bootout + remove plist) on post-install verify failure (MUST 8); deterministic port resolution `--port` > env > `serve.md` > default with a pre-bootstrap socket bind probe. Exposure is **guide-not-perform** (spec/37 boundary, MUST 11): `deploy` prints the exact `tailscale serve --bg http://127.0.0.1:<port>` command + cert-prerequisite and warm-up notes when Tailscale is detected, else a perimeter-doc pointer — it never runs `tailscale serve`, configures a perimeter, or terminates TLS. State is the launchd label + plist; `status`/`down` read launchd, no bespoke state file (MUST 2/12). DRAFT spec/49 (`docs/spec/49-deploy.md`) with a 12-MUST Implementer Contract + conformance outline; ~105 deploy tests (every system seam — launchctl, tailscale, socket probe, HTTP — mocked; each behavioral guard pinned by a per-invocation negative control). Built through the arc quality loop: implement + 3 cross-family (Codex + Opus) adversarial rounds. Supervision reboot-persistence on a headless Mac is deferred ([#539](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/539)); the `resolve_port` `os.environ`-swap refactor is deferred ([#560](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/560)).
- **`ConversationBackend` Protocol + `FilesystemConversationBackend` reference impl (issue [535](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/535) PR 1 of N — the twentieth backend Protocol, v1.5).** New `atomic_agents/conversation/` package: `ConversationBackend` `runtime_checkable` Protocol (spec/47 DRAFT, 10-MUST Implementer Contract); `Principal` frozen dataclass (`identifier`, `derivation_source`, `is_verified`) with `LOCAL_PRINCIPAL = Principal("local", "local", True)` as the home-user zero-config default; `Turn` frozen dataclass (`role`, `content`, `ts`, `run_id`, `seq`, `schema_version`); `ConversationCapabilities` (`supports_principal_isolation`, `supports_token_budget_load`, `supports_canonical_export`, `single_host_only`); `FilesystemConversationBackend` reference impl (layout: `<agent_root>/conversations/<principal_id>/<conversation_id>/<iso_ts>_<run_id>_<NN>_<role>.json` — the `<NN>` zero-padded per-call `seq` disambiguates the user turn (seq=00) and assistant turn (seq=01) which share one `run_id` AND `ts`, so the assistant file no longer overwrites the user file; per-principal exclusive flock write lock; crash-safe `atomic_write()` turn files; oldest-first eviction from a budget-bounded window; Guard (1) `safe_resolve_under(principal_dir, conv_root)` PERIMETER guard (path-escape outside `conversations/`) + Guard (2) a two-part identity guard for cross-principal isolation: (a) `principal_dir.resolve().name == principal.identifier` resolved-basename comparison catches symlink redirection (a sibling symlink `bob -> alice` PASSES Guard (1); only Guard (2a) blocks it), AND (b) a new `_ondisk_principal_name(conversations_root, identifier)` inode-identity check (`st_ino`, `st_dev`) finds the REAL on-disk directory entry and DENIES (`ConversationAccessDenied`) when the on-disk name differs in bytes from the requested identifier — closing a verified cross-principal leak on case-insensitive (macOS APFS) and unicode-normalizing (NFC/NFD) filesystems where `Path.resolve()` returns the caller's spelling, not the on-disk name, so `alice` could read+write `Alice`; both parts pinned by strip negative controls (hardlinks remain a documented inherent-FS not-defended limitation); conv_dir pre-staged symlink containment before `mkdir`; the per-entry Layer-2 canonical-path guard in `write_turn()` is scoped to the per-conversation `conv_dir` (NOT the `conversations/` root) so a custom caller passing a `turn.ts`/`turn.role` with leading `../` cannot land the turn file in a sibling principal/conversation subtree (the unvalidated filename-component injection class — one canonical-path invariant catches it); `export()` iterates principal directories explicitly and re-runs the per-principal IDENTITY guard before enumerating each subtree (NOT a flat `conversations/`-rooted `rglob()`), so a redirecting symlink `conversations/bob -> conversations/alice` is skipped rather than aliasing alice's turns into bob's exported namespace — closing a Python-3.13+ `rglob`-follows-directory-symlinks double-count; stale `.tmp` files excluded from `load_turns()` by `*.json` glob; UTC timestamp normalization in filenames — `Z`-suffix and `+00:00` forms normalized to the same byte pattern so lexicographic order == chronological order; corrupted turn files raise `ConversationCorrupted` — branch-distinctive typed exception skipped with WARNING and `run_id` logged; `load_turns()` validates each parsed turn's schema (role in `{"user","assistant"}`, `content` is `str`, `seq` is a non-negative non-bool `int`) and raises `ConversationCorrupted` (skip+WARN) on violation, so a malformed/maliciously-written turn file cannot inject an out-of-contract provider role (defense-in-depth); O(n) eviction replacing previous O(n²) `list.insert(0, …)` loop). **`agent.call()` full wiring:** three-channel backend selection (constructor kwarg wins → `ATOMIC_AGENTS_CONVERSATION_BACKEND` env → model.md `# Conversation Backend` field PROVISIONAL — all channels resolve to `None` when unset preserving today's single-shot default, rule 14). Channel 3 (`get_conversation_backend(id)` → class → `cls(agent_root)`, distinct from channel 2's `get_default_conversation_backend(agent_root)` → instance) is now covered end-to-end; the coverage surfaced and fixed a wiring bug where `_load_config()` parsed `conversation_backend_id` from `model.md` into the profile snapshot but dropped it when building `AgentConfig`, so channel 3 could never fire (`self.config.conversation_backend_id` always read `None`); prior turns injected as real role-tagged message dicts in `messages[]` BEFORE `work_item` (8000-token conservative budget; TODO model-aware derivation deferred to LOCK PR), normalized before injection (drop empty-content turns, collapse consecutive same-role entries, drop a trailing user turn, AND drop leading assistant turns — the symmetric case to the trailing-user drop: budget eviction is newest-first, so a mid-pair cut can leave an assistant turn first, which the provider rejects) so the provider API never sees a same-role collision, an empty content block, or a leading-assistant message; load AND write-back catch BOTH `ConversationBackendError` AND `PathTraversalError` (a malformed `conversation_id` degrades to single-shot / sets `continuity_persisted=False` instead of crashing the billed call); turn write-back AFTER `_log(log_record)` (JSONL-first principle); `response.continuity_persisted = False` set on write-back failure (non-fatal — LLM call succeeded and is billed) AND on the mid-loop cost-cap skip (no write-back ran); the implicit body-hash idempotency-key auto-derivation now carries an added `and conversation_id is None` condition — auto body-hash dedup is SKIPPED when `conversation_id` is set, because the implicit hash covers only work_item+model+max_tokens+temperature and OMITS prior conversation turns, so deduping a conversation call would replay a stale / cross-conversation result (explicit caller-supplied keys still work); `conversation_id` tagged on ALL seven terminal JSONL record sites (ok, dedup, lock_busy, pre-loop cost-skip, in_flight, mid-loop cost-skip, security-abort paths). **SQLite + Postgres `LogBackend` schema v2→v3 migration:** both add `conversation_id TEXT` column + `idx_conversation_id` partial WHERE NOT NULL index; SQLite migration PRAGMA-guarded for crash-resumability; both `_SCHEMA_VERSION` constants move from 2 to 3; spec/22 versioned normative `conversation_id` addendum. Both backends carry a v2-SEEDED gate-isolation migration test (`test_sqlite_v2_to_v3_migration_gate_isolation` + the Postgres companion) that opens a DB at EXACTLY `schema_version=2` and asserts the v2→v3 block fires while the v1→v2 block does NOT re-run — a v1-seeded ladder test would still pass if the `== 2` gate were mis-changed to `== 1`, so this pins the gate on the backend (SQLite) where it runs locally. **`ConversationExport`** (spec/40 companion) re-exported from `atomic_agents/export/` and `atomic_agents/export/types.py`. **`ConversationBackendError`**, **`ConversationCorrupted`**, and **`ConversationAccessDenied`** added to `atomic_agents/exceptions.py`. **`LogQuery.conversation_id`** AND-predicate field added to `atomic_agents/logs/types.py` AND wired in all three `LogBackend.query()` paths (SQLite `WHERE conversation_id = ?`, Postgres `WHERE conversation_id = %s`, Filesystem skip-predicate) — the column + index ship WITH a real predicate, guarded by a round-trip conformance test with a strip negative control so the filter cannot regress to silently returning all records. **`response.continuity_persisted: bool`** field added to `AtomicAgentResponse`. **DRAFT spec/47** (`docs/spec/47-conversation-backend.md`) with 10-MUST Implementer Contract (MUST 1: side-effect-free construction; MUST 2: principal-scoped fail-closed isolation; MUST 3: path traversal guard on all inputs; MUST 4: atomic turn writes; MUST 5: honest capability declaration; MUST 6: assembly slot is messages[], not system prompt; MUST 7: write-back failure is non-fatal; MUST 8: budget-bounded deterministic load; MUST 9: backward-compatible None default; MUST 10: spec/40 Exportable companion). 107 new tests: 67 conversation conformance (incl. the MUST 3 `turn.run_id` bare-component guard + the MUST 3 `turn.ts` escape-cannot-leak-conv_dir parametrized guard with a conv_root-scoped strip negative control, and the MUST 10 export cross-principal-symlink isolation with a per-principal-guard strip negative control — each with a documented per-invocation negative control) + 17 filesystem-specific + 21 agent.call() wiring (`tests/test_conversation_agent_wiring.py` — turn injection into messages[], same-call user+assistant seq survival, messages[] normalization incl. leading-assistant + trailing-user drops, write-back failure / continuity_persisted incl. the conversation-id-requested-but-no-backend case, mid-loop cost-skip conversation_id tagging, three-channel resolution incl. channel-3 model.md end-to-end + the `parse_model_md_text` provisional-parser unit pin + the misconfigured-env-var fail-soft path — each with a per-invocation negative control) + 2 shared `LogBackend` conformance tests (the `LogQuery.conversation_id` AND-predicate filter + round-trip, parametrized across all reference backends, with a strip negative control proving the WHERE/skip clause is load-bearing). Registry + pgvector wiring and doctor check deferred to later PRs.
- **Cross-principal isolation hardened against case-insensitive / NFC-NFD filesystem aliasing (535).** `_verify_principal_directory` no longer trusts `principal_dir.resolve().name` alone — a new `_ondisk_principal_name(conversations_root, identifier)` resolves the REAL on-disk directory entry by inode identity (`st_ino`, `st_dev`) and raises `ConversationAccessDenied` when the on-disk name differs in bytes from the requested identifier. Closes a verified leak where `Path.resolve()` returns the caller's spelling (not the on-disk name) on macOS APFS (case-fold) and unicode-normalizing filesystems, letting `alice` read+write `Alice`. Hardlinks remain a documented inherent-FS not-defended limitation.
- **Idempotency dedup correctness for conversation calls (535).** The implicit body-hash idempotency-key auto-derivation in `agent.call()` now skips when `conversation_id` is set (`and conversation_id is None`). The implicit hash covers only work_item+model+max_tokens+temperature and OMITS prior conversation turns, so auto-deduping a conversation call would replay a stale / cross-conversation result. Explicit caller-supplied idempotency keys are unaffected.
- **Load-side turn-schema validation (535, defense-in-depth).** `load_turns()` now validates each parsed turn (role in `{"user","assistant"}`, `content` is `str`, `seq` is a non-negative non-bool `int`) and raises `ConversationCorrupted` (skip+WARN) on violation, preventing a malformed or maliciously-written turn file from injecting an out-of-contract provider role.

- **`PrincipalBackend` Protocol + `LocalPrincipalBackend` + `StaticClaimsPrincipalBackend` reference impls (issue [556](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/556) PR 1 of N — the twenty-first backend Protocol, v1.5).** New `atomic_agents/principal/` package: `PrincipalBackend` `runtime_checkable` Protocol (spec/48 DRAFT, 12-MUST Implementer Contract — the 8-MUST PersonaBackend base at positions 1,3,4,5,6,7,8,9 PLUS four named security-boundary MUSTs interleaved at positions 2,10,11,12: never-trust-raw-header/never-re-verify (MUST 2), fail-closed-at-the-gate (MUST 10), storage-key stability + non-reassignability (MUST 11), is_verified honesty (MUST 12)); `PrincipalCapabilities` frozen dataclass (`backend_id` required-first, `is_local_only=False`, `supports_token_verification=False`, `produces_verified_principals=False`); `Principal` and `LOCAL_PRINCIPAL` re-exported from `atomic_agents/conversation/types.py` (NEVER redefined in `principal/`; `is not LOCAL_PRINCIPAL` identity checks throughout the framework depend on the single definition). Two reference impls: **`LocalPrincipalBackend`** (home-user default, `backend_id="local"`, always returns `LOCAL_PRINCIPAL`, `is_local_only=True`, side-effect-free construction, ignores ALL claims) and **`StaticClaimsPrincipalBackend`** (serve-layer multi-user impl, `backend_id="static_claims"`, maps `{provider, sub}` claims to a 64-char sha256 hex identifier via `sha256(f'{provider.strip(_ASCII_WS)}\x00{sub.strip(_ASCII_WS)}'.encode('utf-8')).hexdigest()` (provider/sub stripped of surrounding ASCII whitespace ONLY — `_ASCII_WS = " \t\n\r\f\v"`, NOT `str.strip()`'s Unicode set — so an accidental trailing space cannot split a principal's namespace while two distinct subjects differing only by a leading Unicode space do NOT collide) — NUL-byte separator prevents prefix-collision attacks where `('google','user')` and `('goo','gleuser')` would otherwise alias to the same identifier; absent or malformed claims → `Principal(identifier='anonymous', derivation_source='static_claims', is_verified=False)` — NEVER raises). **`PrincipalBackendError(AtomicAgentsError)`** + **`UnverifiedPrincipalConversationAccess(AtomicAgentsError)`** added to `atomic_agents/exceptions.py`; `UnverifiedPrincipalConversationAccess` carries `conversation_id` and `principal_id` attributes; serve layer maps it → HTTP 401. **`check_principal_backend()`** added to `atomic_agents/doctor.py` AND wired into `run_doctor()` (appended to the backend-checks list alongside `check_idempotency_backend()`, so it actually runs in `atomic-agents doctor` — not dead code) with a dual probe + fail-closed NEGATIVE probe (construction probe + positive probe + negative probe that feeds `{}` empty claims and asserts `is_verified=False`; the negative probe is skipped ONLY for `is_local_only=True` backends — LocalPrincipalBackend always returns `LOCAL_PRINCIPAL` with `is_verified=True` by design; a naive negative probe would false-FAIL it; result name `principal-backend`, FAILs on an unknown `ATOMIC_AGENTS_PRINCIPAL_BACKEND`). **Registry:** `register_principal_backend`, `unregister_principal_backend`, `get_principal_backend`, `list_principal_backends`, `get_default_principal_backend`; `ATOMIC_AGENTS_PRINCIPAL_BACKEND` env var: absent/empty → `LocalPrincipalBackend()`, known id → that backend, unknown id → raises `BackendNotRegistered` LOUDLY (does NOT silently degrade — a misconfigured org deployment must fail fast, distinct from ConversationBackend's soft-degrade). **`agent.call()` wiring — three changes:** (1) `principal_backend: PrincipalBackend | None = None` kwarg added to `AtomicAgent.__init__()` (defaults to `get_default_principal_backend()`; constructor kwarg wins over env var); (2) `principal: Principal = LOCAL_PRINCIPAL` keyword-only arg added to `call()`; (3) **HARD-REFUSE gate** placed BEFORE the idempotency dedup lookup (Phase-1 `lookup()` COMPLETED short-circuit), BEFORE lock acquisition, BEFORE cost gate, BEFORE `begin()` idempotency lease — gate condition: `if conversation_id is not None and not principal.is_verified: raise UnverifiedPrincipalConversationAccess(...)`. **Security ordering (MUST 10):** the gate fires BEFORE the dedup short-circuit so an unverified caller that supplies BOTH a `conversation_id` AND an `idempotency_key` mapping to a COMPLETED ledger record is REFUSED rather than served the victim's cached `result_ref` + `replayed_run_id` — closing a cross-principal run-reference disclosure (the dedup short-circuit serves only callers that already passed the identity gate). Gate keys on `is_verified` boolean ONLY, never on object identity with `LOCAL_PRINCIPAL` — a fabricated `Principal(identifier='local', is_verified=False)` is refused even though its identifier matches. On refuse: best-effort JSONL record with `status='principal_not_verified'` written before raise. On ok path: `principal_id` stamped on JSONL ONLY when `principal is not LOCAL_PRINCIPAL` (backward-compatible — existing home-user logs have no `principal_id` field). **Serve layer HYBRID flow (spec/48 + spec/37) — fail-closed opt-in:** the serve layer NEVER trusts the raw, client-settable identity header as a verified claim by fiat. A new `ServeConfig.identity_is_perimeter_verified` flag (default **False**, operator opt-in via serve.md `# Identity Is Perimeter Verified` or `ATOMIC_AGENTS_SERVE_IDENTITY_PERIMETER_VERIFIED`) gates the raw-header→verified-claim translation; it is additionally ANDed with a non-loopback bind (`is_loopback(bind_host)` — a loopback dev server has no perimeter in front of it), resolved once at `make_app()` as `_identity_perimeter_verified`. `run_agent_call()` in `serve/_runner.py` accepts `verified_claims: dict | None`, `identity_perimeter_verified: bool`, and `conversation_id: str | None`; inside `_call()` closure, after `AtomicAgent` construction, THREE cases: (1) `caller_identity is None AND not identity_perimeter_verified` → `LOCAL_PRINCIPAL` (home-user; the `not identity_perimeter_verified` conjunct is security-load-bearing — under perimeter-trust a header-less request must NOT collapse to the shared verified `local` namespace, so it falls through to the fail-closed branch); (2) identity present but perimeter-trust NOT enabled (default, or loopback), OR perimeter-trust ON but the identity header is absent → a fail-closed `Principal(is_verified=False)` constructed DIRECTLY (not via the registered backend, so the posture cannot be re-opened by a registered `LocalPrincipalBackend` returning a verified principal) → a `conversation_id` caller is HARD-REFUSED; (3) identity present AND perimeter-trust enabled → `agent.principal_backend.derive_principal(verified_claims)`, UNLESS the registered backend reports `capabilities().is_local_only` (cross-tenant collapse guard: the operator turned on perimeter-trust but left the default `LocalPrincipalBackend`, which would IGNORE the claims and return `LOCAL_PRINCIPAL` for every distinct caller, mixing tenants) — in which case the runner mints `Principal(is_verified=False, derivation_source='serve_local_backend_misconfig')` so the HARD-REFUSE gate fires instead of leaking. `serve/_app.py` extracts `conversation_id` from request body JSON (same validation as `idempotency_key`: bare component, no separators, no control chars, max 512 chars; 422 on violation), builds `verified_claims = {'provider': 'http', 'sub': raw_identity}` ONLY when `raw_identity is not None AND _identity_perimeter_verified` — using the FULL untruncated `raw_identity` as the authz `sub`, NOT the 512-char-truncated `caller_identity` (truncating the authz key would collide two distinct subjects sharing the first 512 chars onto one storage identifier; `caller_identity` stays capped at 512 for the AUDIT log only) — and maps `UnverifiedPrincipalConversationAccess` → HTTP 401 with `{status: error, conversation_id, principal_id}`. **Stable-subject guidance (MUST 11):** operators enabling perimeter-trust MUST configure a STABLE subject header (e.g. IAP's `X-Goog-Authenticated-User-ID`), not the default rotating signed-JWT assertion, or the derived storage key changes on every token refresh. **spec/40 Export Exemption recorded:** `PrincipalCapabilities` carries NO `supports_canonical_export` field — PrincipalBackend is an identity-derivation Protocol, not a state store. **Clarifying NOTE added to spec/47 (non-normative):** explains how threading a non-local Principal through `agent.call()` connects to the `principal` parameter in `load_turns()` / `write_turn()` (spec/47's `Principal` type was already present for exactly this extension; no normative rule in spec/47 changes). **Security review (Claude adversarial + cross-family Codex) added four fail-closed fixes to the serve/principal seam, each with a strip-RED-verified negative-control test:** (1) **cross-tenant collapse guard** (`serve/_runner.py` case 3) — perimeter-trust ON + registered backend is `is_local_only` (operator forgot `ATOMIC_AGENTS_PRINCIPAL_BACKEND`) → runner mints `is_verified=False`, `derivation_source='serve_local_backend_misconfig'` so the HARD-REFUSE gate fires, instead of `LocalPrincipalBackend` collapsing every distinct verified caller onto `LOCAL_PRINCIPAL`; (2) **storage-key ASCII-only strip** (`principal/static_claims.py`) — `(provider, sub)` derivation strips ASCII whitespace ONLY (`_ASCII_WS`), NOT `str.strip()`'s Unicode set, so two subjects differing only by a leading Unicode space (NBSP, ideographic space) do NOT collide (was a MUST 11 violation; code now matches spec's documented "ASCII whitespace" intent); (3) **full-identity authz sub** (`serve/_app.py`) — `verified_claims["sub"]` uses the FULL untruncated `raw_identity`, NOT the 512-char-truncated `caller_identity` (reusing the truncated audit value collided two subjects sharing the first 512 chars onto one storage key); `caller_identity` stays capped at 512 for the AUDIT log only; (4) **header-less fail-closed in perimeter mode** (`serve/_runner.py` case 1) — under perimeter-trust, a request OMITTING the identity header now produces an UNVERIFIED principal (hard-refused on `conversation_id`) instead of `LOCAL_PRINCIPAL`; the case (1) guard is now `caller_identity is None AND not identity_perimeter_verified`. The home shape (perimeter OFF + no header) still gets `LOCAL_PRINCIPAL`, unchanged. **Tests (129 new collected — 120 in the three principal files + 9 in modified files):** `tests/test_principal_protocol_conformance.py` (73 collected, parametrized over [LocalPrincipalBackend, StaticClaimsPrincipalBackend] — Protocol structural conformance, fail-closed MUST 1+3 (each with per-invocation negative control), derive_principal return type, storage-key normative encoding with 64-char hex verification and NUL-separator prefix-collision test, the strip-normalization whitespace test + the exported `derive_storage_key` helper-matches-`derive_principal` parity test (closes the off-by-whitespace footgun), capabilities honesty, construction side-effect-free, backend_id stability, is_verified gate invariant); `tests/test_principal_local_impl.py` (19 collected — claims-ignore correctness, singleton identity, construction, capabilities shape, negative-probe-skipped-by-design documentation, registry round-trip, env-var dispatch, fail-loud-on-unknown-env-var with per-invocation negative control); `tests/test_principal_call_wiring.py` (28 collected — HARD-REFUSE raises `UnverifiedPrincipalConversationAccess`, gate fires BEFORE `_dispatch_with_judge` (patched to assert not called), verified principal passes gate, LOCAL_PRINCIPAL passes gate, unverified principal WITHOUT conversation_id NOT refused, gate keys on is_verified NOT object identity (fabricated-local-identifier test), exception carries conversation_id+principal_id, the security-refusal JSONL audit record is persisted+queryable (`status='principal_not_verified'` with run_id+conversation_id+idempotency_key+principal_id+http_caller) with a per-invocation negative control proving the assertion is load-bearing on the real `self._log(...)` write, no-conv-backend STILL refuses, AtomicAgent.principal_backend attribute, default is LocalPrincipalBackend, accepts custom kwarg, kwarg wins over env var, storage-key determinism/non-reassignability/normative-example, conversation isolation (different principals), exception hierarchy, PrincipalBackendError not-raised-for-malformed-claims, plus the cross-tenant `serve_local_backend_misconfig` refusal, the storage-key non-reassignability strip test, and the header-absent-under-perimeter fail-closed test) + 9 net-new in modified files (2 test_doctor, 4 test_serve_app incl. full-identity-not-truncated `sub`, 3 test_serve_config). A raw-token (pyjwt/jose) self-verifying backend + the `[auth]` extra, role-based authorization, and full serve multi-tenant integration deferred to follow-ups.

- **ConversationBackend test-coverage backfill (issue [557](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/557) — polish).** 31 new tests across `test_conversation_agent_wiring.py` and `test_conversation_filesystem.py` closing the 84% coverage gaps from PR1. No production code changed. **(1) 5 tag-site test pairs** in `test_conversation_agent_wiring.py` — one positive/negative pair per untested terminal record site: pre-loop cost-skip (status='skipped', LLM must NOT fire), lock_busy (pre-holds the fake lock; asserts exactly one lock_busy record), dedup/COMPLETED (idempotency_backend.lookup → COMPLETED short-circuit; LLM must NOT fire), in_flight (lookup→FRESH, begin→IN_FLIGHT; asserts DedupInFlight + exactly one in_flight record), security-abort/MCPCommandNotAllowed (injects a fake MCPServerSpec + patches MCPClientPool.connect_all to raise MCPCommandNotAllowed; identifies the abort record by its full shape — status + error + summary prefix — not a loose filter). Each test has a per-invocation negative control: same terminal path WITHOUT conversation_id asserts the field ABSENT. Process-state isolation autouse fixture snapshots/restores MCPClientPool module-global + ATOMIC_AGENTS_CONVERSATION_BACKEND env to prevent cross-module flakes. **(2) filesystem-specific tests** — `_normalize_utc_ts` tz-naive WARNING branch (feed a tz-naive-but-valid ISO ts with no offset; assert the 'no timezone; treating as UTC' WARNING + +00:00 result + the time component unchanged; negative control: UTC-aware ts must NOT emit the warning); 5 I/O-error mapping tests with selective patches so only the INTENDED sink fires (glob OSError→ConversationBackendError, ENOENT read_text→TOCTOU-skip with two-test layered-except split asserting distinct outcomes, non-ENOENT OSError→ConversationBackendError, principal mkdir OSError, lock-file open OSError; atomic_write OSError); export iterdir OSError→ConversationBackendError; os.scandir OSError→PathTraversalError (pinning that _ondisk_principal_name maps to PathTraversalError, not ConversationBackendError). **(3) registry helpers** — list_conversation_backends (lexicographic sort), unregister (removes + subsequent get raises BackendNotRegistered), unregister no-op when absent, get_conversation_backend BackendNotRegistered, re-register logs DEBUG, get_default registry-dispatch (tests the non-'filesystem' code path explicitly), get_default fail-fast on unknown id, _redact_for_error_message (4 shapes: URL-scheme, schemeless DSN, truncation with truncation-specific assertion, short passthrough). Registry tests use a snapshot/restore _isolated_conv_registry fixture mirroring sibling backend registry patterns. - **`EmbeddingBackend` Protocol + `OpenAIEmbeddingBackend` reference impl (issue [200](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/200) PR 2 of 3 — the nineteenth backend Protocol, v1.5).** New `atomic_agents/embedding/` package: `EmbeddingBackend` `runtime_checkable` Protocol (spec/46 DRAFT, 9-MUST Implementer Contract); `EmbeddingCapabilities` frozen dataclass (`max_batch_size`, `max_input_tokens`, `supports_input_type`); `OpenAIEmbeddingBackend` reference impl (`text-embedding-3-small` default; configurable `model_id` + `dimensions` — an omitted `dimensions` resolves to the model's NATIVE size via a per-model native-dimension map, so 3-large defaults to 3072, not a global 1536; per-call `_build_client()` for test isolation; key resolution DELEGATES to the framework SecretBackend (spec/38) via the same `_llm._get_key` resolver as `OpenAICompatibleLLMBackend`, so whatever backend is registered (Filesystem env/Keychain/keys.json, GCP Secret Manager, …) resolves the key — the embedding path is NOT a private cascade that bypasses an operator's `ATOMIC_AGENTS_SECRET_BACKEND`; unresolved → `None` so construction stays graceful). **MUST-NOT-RAISE invariant:** `embed()` and `embed_batch()` return `None` on any failure (network, rate-limit, token-length, auth) — never raise. **len(out)==len(in) conformance invariant:** `embed_batch()` always returns a list of exactly `len(texts)` elements; failed items produce `None` at their index. **`EmbeddingError(AtomicAgentsError)` + `EmbeddingProviderUnavailable(EmbeddingError)`** added to `atomic_agents/exceptions.py` — for internal logging only; `embed()` must not raise them. **`EMBEDDING_PRICING` cost table** isolated from `PRICING` (chat models) in `atomic_agents/_costs.py`; `calc_embedding_cost(model_id, input_tokens) → (cost_usd, cost_estimated: bool)` uses `_embedding_fallback_rate()` which scans ONLY `EMBEDDING_PRICING` (max ~$0.13/1M, NOT Opus's $75/1M); rates verified 2026-06-17 against OpenAI's authoritative per-model docs pages (text-embedding-3-small $0.02, 3-large $0.13, ada-002 $0.10), each row annotated with its source URL. `cost_estimated=True` means "unpriced model, used fallback rate" — distinct from `degraded=True` (I/O failure). `input_tokens` is sanity-guarded: a non-integer (NaN/inf float, bool) is treated as 0 (a NaN reservation would fail-open against a cap, an inf would block every call), and an implausibly large count is capped at `_MAX_EMBEDDING_INPUT_TOKENS` so the float cost math cannot overflow to `inf`. **`supports_input_type` flag advertised, parameter deferred:** all PR 2 implementations advertise `supports_input_type=False`; the `input_type` kwarg on the Protocol surface ships in PR 3 when flag semantics are confirmed. **No production registry in PR 2** — constructor-injected by consuming backends; registry + pgvector wiring shipped PR3 (#200); `check_embedding_backend()` doctor check shipped 544 PR1. **Dimension honesty (MUST 3) enforced at three layers:** (1) for `text-embedding-3-*` (reducible) a non-native `dimensions` is forwarded and honored; a value ABOVE native is REFUSED at construction (reduction can only shrink, never grow — a larger value would advertise a length the API never produces); (2) for `text-embedding-ada-002` (no server-side reduction) any non-native `dimensions` is REFUSED at construction; (3) as a backstop for any mismatch that escapes construction (unknown model whose true native size differs from the fallback, or provider/SDK drift), `embed()`/`embed_batch()` verify `len(vector) == dimensions` on the produced vector and return `None` (never a wrong-length vector). **Provider-availability errors take a reachable typed branch:** `embed()`/`embed_batch()` map known OpenAI SDK availability errors to `EmbeddingProviderUnavailable` so the branch-distinctive "provider unavailable" WARNING fires. Detection uses TWO sets, because in the real openai SDK (verified against 2.35.1) EVERY API exception inherits the SDK root `OpenAIError`: a leaf-EXACT set `{OpenAIError}` matched on `type(exc).__name__` (catching ONLY the bare root raised at client construction when no key is resolvable — not every subclass that inherits it), plus an MRO-membership set (`AuthenticationError`, `RateLimitError`, `APIConnectionError`, `APITimeoutError`, `InternalServerError` 5xx — the genuine availability leaf classes, so their subclasses still match). `PermissionDeniedError` (403) is deliberately EXCLUDED from the availability set — a 403 is a PERSISTENT operator-actionable misconfiguration (billing/access/scope), not a transient outage, so it takes the broad "unexpected error" branch where the honest label points the operator at their config. 4xx CLIENT errors (`BadRequestError` 400 / `NotFoundError` 404 / `UnprocessableEntityError` 422 — e.g. input-too-long) likewise hit the broad branch. (A naive any-ancestor match against a set containing `OpenAIError` would mislabel all of them, since `OpenAIError` is their shared root; the leaf-exact split is what prevents it. A live-SDK guard test pins the real hierarchy so the test fakes — which now model the full `BadRequestError → APIStatusError → APIError → OpenAIError` chain — cannot drift from it.) **`embed_batch()` response validation:** short-circuits a provider-unavailable batch to `[None] * len(texts)` directly (no doomed per-item retry amplification); a STRUCTURALLY malformed response (duplicate or out-of-range index, or a wrong-length vector — the silent-wrong-vector class for a vector store) degrades the WHOLE batch to per-item `embed()`; a well-formed but INCOMPLETE response re-embeds ONLY the missing indices (no re-billing of items the batch already returned). Branch phrases are asserted by tests with negative controls. **Construction input validation (MUST 1):** empty/whitespace `model_id` or non-positive `dimensions` raise `EmbeddingError` at `__init__`; an unknown (unlisted) model with a non-native explicit `dimensions` is refused with a message that does NOT assert a native size the backend does not actually know (spec/46 MUST 3 unknown-model limitation). **`/ship` review-army hardening (past arc convergence, cross-family Codex + Red Team):** the SecretBackend delegation (was a private cascade bypassing GCP Secret Manager — split-brain credentials), the reducible-over-native refusal + produced-length backstop (was a silent-None-forever / pgvector phantom-column path), the `embed_batch` dup/out-of-range/wrong-length validation (was silent vector corruption), the 403-as-config-error reclassification, and the cost overflow/NaN guards were all caught by the review army after the arc's 5 rounds and fixed inline with negative controls. **Tests (129 new):** `tests/stub_embedding.py` (TEST-ONLY `StubEmbeddingBackend` + `_RaisingStubEmbeddingBackend` for negative controls); `tests/test_embedding_protocol_conformance.py` (55 tests, parametrized over [stub, openai-mocked], including typed-branch + MUST-1 + dimensions + MUST-9 truncation negative controls per `feedback_false_green_test_needs_per_invocation_negative_control`, a mechanized guard asserting production code never imports the test stub, and an autouse hermetic fixture that fails loudly if a classifier monkeypatch leaks across the test boundary); `tests/test_openai_embedding.py` (53 OpenAI impl-specific tests, 3 skipped pending `ATOMIC_AGENTS_TEST_OPENAI_KEY` integration marker — incl. SecretBackend-delegation tests (spec triple + real FilesystemSecretBackend resolution + graceful-None-on-unresolved catching the base `AtomicAgentsError`), reducible-over-native construction refusal + negative control + at/below-native boundary, produced-length-mismatch → None, `embed_batch` duplicate/out-of-range/wrong-length → per-item degrade and incomplete → re-embed-only-the-gap, 403 PermissionDenied → broad branch + negative control, construction-raise MUST-NOT-RAISE regression, 4xx-client-error broad-branch classification parametrized over 400/404/422 + leaf-exact-split negative control + a live-SDK guard, the already-typed-provider-unavailable forward-defense branch, provider-unavailable batch no-amplification, `_normalize_length` units, and an import-time-captured hermetic classifier-pristine fixture); 21 `TestEmbeddingPricing` tests added to `tests/test_costs.py` (table isolation, cross-table contamination guard, rate verification, warn-once dedup, warned-set cap + still-warns, sub-threshold reservation rounds-up-nonzero, negative-token clamp, implausibly-large-token cap, non-integer-token guard). **`PostgresMemoryBackend` docstrings** updated to distinguish EmbeddingBackend Protocol (now shipped) from pgvector wiring (still deferred to 258 PR 2/PR 3). The supersession of spec/34's "no standalone use case" rationale is recorded in spec/46 (DRAFT) only — the LOCKED spec/34 is NOT edited in PR 2; its normative addendum lands in PR 3 with the pgvector wiring. The spec/46 cost-gate mandate covers BOTH `embed()` (query-embedding sites) and `embed_batch()` (ingestion sites) so PR 3 cannot wire only the batch path and leave query-time embeds as an ungated billable call (Principle 4). Follow-up [534](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/534) tracks a free local embedding backend (sentence-transformers/Ollama). Suite **5112 passed / 136 skipped**.

- **Embed batch cost gate at `agent.call()` capture-commit site + `PRIMITIVE_EMBED` + `check_embedding_backend()` doctor check + spec/20/22/34 normative addenda (issue [544](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/544) PR 1 of 2 — v1.5).** Wires the first Principle-#4-compliant cost guardrail for embedding spend at the `agent.call()` orchestrator layer — the only site where all four Principle-4 conditions are met (cap context, cost history, tool registry, and the billing decision). **`PRIMITIVE_EMBED = 'embed'`** added to `atomic_agents/logs/types.py` (distinct from `PRIMITIVE_HELPER` — embedding bills from `EMBEDDING_PRICING`, not chat `PRICING`; folding into `helper` would make a `GROUP BY primitive` attribution query permanently ambiguous). **Four JSONL audit triggers** (`embed_batch_reservation`, `embed_batch_release`, `embed_reservation`, `embed_release`) wired in `_PRIMITIVE_BY_TRIGGER` in `atomic_agents/logs/__init__.py`; all four map to `PRIMITIVE_EMBED`. **Batch embed cost gate** placed at the `agent.call()` post-loop capture-commit site (after all tool calls, inside the write-note block that fires when `_embed_gate_active` and captures are present): (1) worst-case reservation emits `embed_batch_reservation` before the `write_note()` batch loop; (2) release in `finally` emits `embed_batch_release` with actual cost (per-written-note byte-token estimate; 0 only when no notes were written; `actual_usd` is NOT conditioned on whether `embed()` returned `None` — `write_note()` does not surface that signal, so a successfully-written note is charged the full per-note estimate); (3) gate raises `CostGuardrailBlocked` (with `embed_batch_blocked=True` marker) when worst-case reservation exceeds `_embed_remaining_headroom()` — the cap is ENFORCED, not audit-only; (4) a terminal `CostGuardrailBlocked` JSONL record with `status=error` + `embed_batch_blocked` + the billed chat `cost_usd` is written on refusal so the spend is never silent in the ledger (Principle 5 + the 495/497/498 under-counting guard). **`_has_effective_embed_cap()`** resolves the full Policy + model.md + tree-cap hierarchy (NOT model.md-only) to determine whether the fail-closed predicate applies; `cost_estimated=True` (unknown embedding model, fallback rate) does NOT gate — only `CostReadResult.degraded AND effective_cap is not None` gates fail-closed. **Merge-write reservations are fragment-sized** (per-note byte-token estimate), NOT merged-body-sized — the pre-merge body is unavailable at reservation time; worst-case is 2× per-note sum (batch/per-item fan-out). **`check_embedding_backend()`** added to `atomic_agents/doctor.py` AND wired into `run_doctor()`: SKIP when `ATOMIC_AGENTS_EMBEDDING_BACKEND` is unset (opt-in default); construction probe FAIL on unknown id / import error; key-resolution probe WARN when `_api_key is None` (backend configured but key missing — embeds degrade to `None`); PASS otherwise. No billable `embed()` call is made. **Versioned normative addenda** (each added OUTSIDE the parent spec's MUST count): `spec/20` — `MemoryCapabilities.embedding_provider` is a provider LABEL string (not a model id); `embedding_backend_resolved` SNAPSHOT SECURITY CLAMP (MUST serialize as `None` in any JSONL/snapshot/network response — live instance may carry API credentials); `spec/22` — `PRIMITIVE_EMBED = 'embed'` bucket semantics + 4-trigger table + `embed_batch_reservation`/`embed_batch_release` payload fields + `actual_usd` is fragment-estimated (NOT conditioned on `embed()` return); `embed_reservation`/`embed_release` defined here but NOT yet emitted (query-embed gate deferred to CLI corpus-query site 564, PR2); `spec/34` — `CorpusCapabilities.embedding_backend_resolved` SNAPSHOT SECURITY CLAMP (mirrors spec/20). **Query-embed gate is correctly ABSENT** — `memory.search()` and `corpus.query()` are never called inside `agent.call()` (confirmed by grep over `agent.py`); there is no orchestrator query-embed path to gate; the real ungated query-embed path is the CLI corpus-query command, tracked in [564](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/564), which is the PR2 gate site. spec/46 STAYS DRAFT until #544 PR2. **39 new tests** in `tests/test_embed_cost_gate.py` — gate active/inactive (opt-in default OFF with negative control), reservation/release pair with JSONL assertion, worst-case 2× sizing, actual-usd-zero-when-no-notes, refusal raises `CostGuardrailBlocked` + writes terminal JSONL record, `_has_effective_embed_cap()` Policy+tree-cap resolution, `_embed_remaining_headroom()` headroom arithmetic, doctor SKIP/PASS/WARN/FAIL branches + api_key probe, per-invocation strip negative controls throughout. **`/ship` review-army hardening (past arc convergence, 4 specialists + cross-family Codex):** the doctor typed-FAIL branch tests (EmbeddingError/ImportError/BackendNotRegistered) were layered-except false-greens — they asserted the type name the broad `except Exception` ALSO emits, so they stayed green when the typed branch was stripped; now each asserts its branch-distinctive `fix_hint` (strip-test verified RED), plus a new broad-except branch test (per `feedback_layered_except_typed_branch_false_green`); the `embed_batch_reservation`/`embed_batch_release` records gained the `parent_agent` field every other batch record carries (audit-join correctness, Principle 5); the byte-per-token divisor and the 2× fan-out buffer became named constants and the token estimate a shared `_estimate_embed_tokens()` helper used by both the reservation and true-up loops (cannot desync), and the effective-cap hierarchy was factored into one `_effective_embed_caps()` helper shared by the predicate and the headroom math; the `bytes/3` worst-case wording was softened from "never under-reserves" to "conservative, not a strict upper bound" with the provider per-text token cap as the real bound (spec/46). - **Embed cost accounting fixes — dedicated `embed_cost` record + merge-write pre-read reservation (issue [544](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/544) PR 2a — v1.5).** Two accounting correctness fixes; spec/46 STAYS DRAFT (the query-embed gate and LOCK ceremony remain deferred to PR2). (1) **Cross-call embed accounting:** `embed_batch_release` intentionally carries no `cost_usd` (audit/reconciliation record only). A new dedicated `embed_cost` JSONL record (`trigger='embed_cost'`, `cost_usd=actual_usd`, `cost_source='actor'`, `model`, `cost_estimated`, `parent_run_id`, `parent_agent`) is now emitted in the same `finally` block IMMEDIATELY AFTER the release record, conditioned on `actual_usd > 0`. `'embed_cost': PRIMITIVE_EMBED` added to `_PRIMITIVE_BY_TRIGGER` so `GROUP BY primitive` attribution is correct on disk. `sum_cost_for_period` now sees prior embed spend on subsequent calls, so the gate baseline is accurate. Only the dedicated record carries `cost_usd` — the double-count guard (release + reservation must not carry `cost_usd`) is regression-tested end-to-end against the REAL actor-cost aggregate (`cost_usd` summed over EVERY actor-source record, the same quantity `sum_cost_for_period` folds — NOT a `trigger=='embed_cost'` proxy that would hide a release-record leak), isolating the embed contribution against a matched-token chat baseline so one call adds `actual_usd` exactly once and two calls sum to exactly `2×` (not `4×`); a release/reservation record carrying `cost_usd` reddens it (strip-verified). (2) **Merge-write pre-read reservation:** a merge write PRESERVES the target body verbatim — the backend re-embeds the stored TARGET body ALONE; the incoming fragment updates `sources`/`last_seen` metadata, NOT the body (`backend.py` §write contract: "preserve body verbatim"; `filesystem._merge_into_existing` leaves the content untouched; `pgvector.write_note` embeds `stored.body`). The PR1 gate sized both the reservation and the true-up from the small incoming fragment, under-reserving and under-charging a merge whose stored target body is large. The PR1 entry sized from the fragment because it asserted the pre-merge body was unavailable at reservation time — that rationale was wrong: the target body IS readable via `read_note(merge_into)` before the write loop, and the backend re-embeds the preserved target body alone. Now the reservation loop calls `self.memory.read_note(merge_into)` BEFORE the write loop and sizes the reservation from the PRESERVED TARGET body so an over-cap merge is refused before billing (Principle #4 — enforce-before-pay). On read failure, falls back to fragment-only (conservative-toward-permissive) and logs a WARNING. The pre-read result is cached in `_merge_body_cache` (keyed by the same `(type, name, hash(body), merge_into)` dedup 4-tuple the loops use, so two same-name captures targeting different notes cannot collide) and reused in the true-up loop so `reserve` and `actual_usd` share the same estimate and cannot desync. Write-loop dedup key extended from `(type, name, hash(body))` to `(type, name, hash(body), merge_into)`. `_emit_embed_batch_release` docstring KNOWN LIMITATION block updated to RESOLVED. The `embed_cost` record shape is documented in a spec/22 versioned normative addendum (§"`embed_cost` cross-call accounting record") — a description-of-shipped-behavior addendum following the spec/45 PR2 (520) / spec/22 read-failure (497) precedent (NOT a normative-contract change), because the PR1 addendum's "cost_usd ABSENT / cross-call accounting deferred to PR2" statement would otherwise have LOCKED spec/22 asserting the opposite of shipped behavior (Principle 10/13). The full DRAFT→LOCKED ceremony for spec/46 (and the CLI query-embed gate) remains deferred to PR2; the DRAFT spec/46 carries the in-flight description. **18 new PR2a tests** in `tests/test_embed_cost_gate.py` (57 total in the file: 39 from PR1 + 18 from PR2a) — `embed_cost` trigger routes to `PRIMITIVE_EMBED`; dedicated record emitted after release (ordering invariant); record carries `cost_usd > 0 == actual_usd`; record carries `parent_agent` + `parent_run_id` + `cost_source='actor'`; record not emitted when all writes fail (actual_usd=0 gate); record not emitted for non-semantic backend; `embed_batch_release` still has no `cost_usd`; double-count guard driving the REAL `sum_cost_for_period`/`query()` aggregate over EVERY actor record (NOT a trigger-filtered proxy — a release record leaking `cost_usd` is summed exactly as production would sum it, so the double-count strip-control bites: one capture call folds embed spend in exactly once, two calls fold it twice, and a chat baseline with matched token usage isolates the embed contribution); merge-write reservation/true-up sized from the PRESERVED TARGET body (test fake models the real merge contract: post-merge stored body == target body, fragment NOT accumulated); merge-write over-cap refused when target size > headroom; same-name/different-merge-target captures each trued-up at their own target body (cache-key collision regression); read_note() raises falls back gracefully + logs warning; merge-write empty-target-body and missing-target (read_note→None) both fall back to fragment sizing (the two operands of the `if _stored_note is not None and _stored_note.body` compound guard, each strip-controlled separately per the project's compound-boolean discipline — the empty-body control verified RED when the `.body` operand is dropped); per-invocation negative controls (strip each fix part separately — release-record leak, embed_cost cost_usd strip, merge reservation pre-read, merge true-up cache read, cache-key collision, compound-guard `.body` operand — each verified to go RED). Suite **5445 passed / 136 skipped** (0 failures, 0 regressions). **/ship cross-family review (Codex) hardening:** corrected the `566` KNOWN LIMITATION docstring — the same-batch create-then-merge under-count is NOT bounded by the 2x fan-out buffer (the buffer is sized on the fragment, not the re-embedded target body); it is sub-cent in practice given embedding pricing but a genuine ledger/cap under-count, so `566` was updated with the corrected severity + magnitude. Filed `567` (embed cap headroom excludes the current call's in-flight chat spend) and `568` (spend escapes the cap baseline if the process crashes between the release and `embed_cost` records). Removed a redundant inline `import logging` in the merge pre-read except block (use the module `_logger`); reworded the `embed_cost` summary `({N} notes)` → `(batch of {N})` so a partial batch does not imply all N notes were written. Security / Performance / Testing specialists: NO FINDINGS (Testing strip-verified every negative control RED).

- **`PgvectorMemoryBackend` + `PgvectorCorpusBackend` + EmbeddingBackend registry — pgvector semantic recall wired into the Postgres memory/corpus family (issue [200](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/200) PR 3 of 3 — spec/46 stays DRAFT; cost-gate wiring + LOCK ceremony deferred to [#544](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/544), v1.5).** Advances the #200 arc by wiring the shipped `EmbeddingBackend` Protocol into both memory and corpus backends, **opt-in by default** so no billable embedding spend runs without an explicit operator opt-in. **`PgvectorMemoryBackend`** (`backend_id "pgvector-memory"`, `atomic_agents/memory/pgvector.py`): subclasses `PostgresMemoryBackend` (inherits FTS/versioning/staging/export); adds `memory_note_embeddings` side table (`_SCHEMA_VERSION = 3`, v2→v3 additive migration — canonical `memory_notes` row UNTOUCHED per ruling Q1); HNSW index with `vector_cosine_ops` (created only when `dimensions <= 2000`, pgvector's HNSW limit — above it, e.g. `text-embedding-3-large` at 3072 dims, the index is skipped and ANN degrades to sequential scan rather than crashing schema-init); ANN `search()` with `WHERE model_id=... AND dimensions=...` filter (stale rows from a since-replaced model are excluded from the ANN join; a successful ANN result is authoritative — empty-is-authoritative, no per-query FTS union — so a note whose only embedding is stale-model stays reachable via the FTS-only paths but not via semantic `search()` until re-embedded; FTS fallback fires only when the query embed returns `None` or the ANN query raises); two-phase write (embed OUTSIDE transaction, then UPSERT side table); `supports_semantic_search` property; `capabilities()` returns `MemoryCapabilities` with `embedding_backend_resolved` typed reference. **`PgvectorCorpusBackend`** (`backend_id "pgvector-corpus"`, `atomic_agents/corpus/pgvector.py`): wraps `FilesystemCorpusBackend` (inherits page storage, versioning, atomic writes); adds `corpus_page_embeddings` Postgres embedding index for ANN `query()`; `index_page()` two-phase embed-then-upsert; substring/tag-match fallback when Postgres URL or embedding backend absent. **EmbeddingBackend registry** (`atomic_agents/embedding/registry.py`): five functions (`register_embedding_backend`, `unregister_embedding_backend`, `get_embedding_backend`, `list_embedding_backends`, `get_default_embedding_backend`); `get_default_embedding_backend()` reads `ATOMIC_AGENTS_EMBEDDING_BACKEND/_MODEL/_DIMENSIONS` (the `_URL` var is reserved but not yet read — no shipped backend takes a base-URL kwarg). **Opt-in by default (cost-safety):** semantic search is OFF unless the operator EXPLICITLY pins `ATOMIC_AGENTS_EMBEDDING_BACKEND` (or injects a backend) — an unset/blank var returns `None` → `supports_semantic_search=False` → FTS only. This prevents surprise billable embedding spend merely because an `OPENAI_API_KEY` is reachable in the environment; an explicit pin then fails LOUD on any misconfig (typo → `BackendNotRegistered`, missing `[openai]` extra → `ImportError`, missing/misconfigured credential → `SecretBackendNotRegistered`) rather than silently degrading to FTS. **`input_type` kwarg** added to Protocol `embed()` and `embed_batch()` surfaces (accepted-but-ignored by `OpenAIEmbeddingBackend` — OpenAI SDK verified per Principle 12 not to expose `input_type`; `supports_input_type=False` stays honest). **C2 fail-hard** on missing pgvector extension (`RuntimeError` with clear message; the backend NEVER runs `CREATE EXTENSION` itself — CI's "Enable pgvector extension" step handles it). **Dimension-width fail-hard (cross-family review fix):** the embed call sites (`write_note`/`search`/`index_page`/`query`) fail loud — once per instance, BEFORE the billable embed — if the existing `vector(N)` side-table column width doesn't match the active model's dimensions (e.g. a side table created at the FTS-only 1536 default, then a 3072 model pinned), so an operator never pays for embeds that silently fail to store/query against a mismatched column. Guarding at the embed site (not schema-init) leaves the construct-then-reprovision flow — a migration, or a DROP-and-recreate — unblocked. Both memory and corpus backends carry the guard. **[pgvector] extra** and `pgvector/pgvector:pg16` CI image added. **`MemoryCapabilities` dataclass** (`embedding_provider: str|None`, `embedding_backend_resolved: EmbeddingBackend|None`) and **`CorpusCapabilities.embedding_backend_resolved`** typed sibling (`EmbeddingBackend|None`, default `None`, backward-compatible) added as CODE this PR; the matching LOCKED spec/20 + spec/34 normative addenda were deferred to [544](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/544) PR1 where they are authored atomically with the gate (per the #201 sibling-field precedent), keeping this PR free of LOCKED-spec edits. **Shipped in 544 PR1 (not this PR):** the live embed cost-gate reservation/release + JSONL audit at the `agent.call()` capture-commit site, `PRIMITIVE_EMBED = 'embed'` + 4 triggers, the `check_embedding_backend()` doctor check, the spec/20 + spec/22 + spec/34 normative addenda. **Still deferred to 544 PR2:** the query-embed gate (CLI corpus-query, [564](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/564)) and the spec/46 DRAFT→LOCKED ceremony. The full-Postgres corpus (so `PgvectorCorpusBackend` pages leave the filesystem) is tracked in [#540](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/540). **Tests:** `tests/test_embedding_registry.py` (18 — registry round-trip, `BackendNotRegistered` error, `get_default_embedding_backend()` env dispatch, **opt-in default returns None even with the SDK + key present** (the cost-safety footgun, with a negative control that reddens if the guard is stripped), blank/whitespace provider treated as unset, malformed `ATOMIC_AGENTS_EMBEDDING_DIMENSIONS` on an explicit pin fails loud (cross-family review fix), explicit-pin fail-loud on typo/missing-extra, `SecretBackendNotRegistered` re-raise); `tests/test_pgvector_memory_backend.py` (31 — 10 always-run: schema/version constants, `_SCHEMA_VERSION=3` guard, subclass assertion, factory import guard + degradation coverage; 21 `requires_postgres`: schema migration, HNSW index, write-stores-embedding, ANN search, ANN model filter, C2 fail-hard, dimension-width mismatch fail-hard, capabilities, close-idempotency, FTS fallback, best-effort write without embedding backend); `tests/test_pgvector_corpus_backend.py` (15 — 12 always-run + 3 `requires_postgres`: schema/index creation, ANN `query()`, ANN model filter, dimension-width mismatch fail-hard, two-phase index-then-upsert, substring fallback when Postgres URL/embedding backend absent); `test_embedding_protocol_conformance.py` (60 — added `test_embed_accepts_input_type_kwarg` + `test_embed_batch_accepts_input_type_kwarg` + `test_capabilities_supports_input_type_is_false` rewording). Suite **4986 passed / 136 skipped** locally (the `requires_postgres` pgvector paths run in CI's `pgvector/pgvector:pg16` lane).

- **`PostgresMemoryBackend` — Postgres FTS/tsvector reference impl for `MemoryBackend` Protocol (issue [258](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/258) PR 1 of 4).** Non-semantic Postgres reference impl conforming to the LOCKED spec/20 MemoryBackend Protocol. Ships alongside `FilesystemBackend` as the second MemoryBackend reference impl; targets multi-host deployments (Cloud Run, shared Postgres). `atomic_agents/memory/postgres.py`; opt-in via `pip install 'atomic-agents-stack[postgres]'`. **Selection:** `ATOMIC_AGENTS_MEMORY_BACKEND=postgres` + `ATOMIC_AGENTS_MEMORY_BACKEND_URL=postgresql://user:passhost:5432/dbname`; lazy-imported on selection (base package startup does NOT load psycopg). **FTS (tsvector) recall — `supports_semantic_search=False`:** `search()` runs full-text search over `tsvector` computed inline on `display_name`, `name`, `description`, `body` columns — NOT semantic/vector search; pgvector + EmbeddingBackend Protocol (#200) are explicitly deferred to PR 2/PR 3. The FTS mode is a non-semantic alternative to filesystem's substring scan, consistent with spec/20 MUST 7 via the project's own interpretation (documented in the spec/20 Postgres addendum §"FTS search (MUST 7 compliance)"). **Schema (`_SCHEMA_VERSION = 2`, INDEPENDENT of `PostgresLogBackend`):** three tables — `memory_notes` (live notes), `memory_note_versions` (version snapshots), `memory_meta` (schema version tracking); uuid-named regular (not TEMPORARY) staging tables for `create_staging()`. v1→v2 migration adds `display_name` (idempotent `ALTER TABLE … ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS`) so the human note name (`capture.name`) round-trips to `Note.name` for cross-backend parity with FilesystemBackend. Advisory lock key (`b'atomic-agents-memory-schema-v1'`) is DISTINCT from the LogBackend key so log and memory cold-start DDL serialize independently even when sharing a database. **MUST 5 WritePolicy (Postgres interpretation):** no filesystem paths — all notes are row-addressed; `agent_root` scope check (authorized when `agent_root` falls under a `write_paths` entry and NOT under any `read_only_paths` entry) is the Postgres-scope equivalent of `FilesystemBackend._enforce_write_path()`, documented in the spec/20 Postgres addendum. **Four write cases** (mirroring FilesystemBackend): Case 1 merge_into, Case 2 fresh INSERT, Case 3 orphan-recovery, Case 4 collision (`SchemaValidationError`). TOCTOU protection: `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` serializes Cases 1/3/4; UNIQUE(name) backstop catches the concurrent Case 2 race (SQLSTATE 23505 → `SchemaValidationError`, same domain exception as Case 4, no raw psycopg type leaks through the Protocol boundary). CAS precondition (`expected_content_sha256`) available on `write_note()`. **Thread safety:** `threading.local` per-thread connection + `threading.Lock`-guarded global list so `close()` from the main thread releases all worker-thread connections (required for `helper_call_parallel()` worker threads). **Cold-start:** `pg_advisory_xact_lock` inside a transaction serializes multi-replica DDL. **Credential redaction:** three layers (URL stripping via `_redact_dsn()`, explicit keyword args to psycopg, `_password` stored for driver use but redacted in all logs/errors). **Tier B field-lossless export** (`supports_canonical_export=True`): `render_note_bytes_from_object(note)` from `atomic_agents/export/renderer.py`; field-level round-trip guaranteed; byte-exact NOT guaranteed (date YAML format + key ordering diverge per spec/40 §"Tier A vs Tier B fidelity"). **DreamRunner limitation:** `DreamRunner` guards against non-filesystem backends at construction (raises `NotImplementedError`); `create_staging()` / `apply_staging()` are implemented but DreamRunner cannot call them until 396 ships. **Doctor:** existing `check_memory_backend_config` / `check_memory_backend` already probe the Postgres backend via the `get_default_memory_backend()` factory; `backend_id == "postgres"` branch in `check_memory_backend_config` provides a Postgres-specific fix hint. **`ATOMIC_AGENTS_MEMORY_BACKEND_URL` companion env var** added to `memory/__init__.py`'s `get_default_memory_backend()` lazy-dispatch branch (mirrors `logs/__init__.py`'s PostgresLogBackend dispatch); `"postgres"` listed in `_LAZY_BACKEND_IDS` so registry-introspection tests show it as a known id before the extra is installed. **spec/20 Postgres addendum** added as a NON-NORMATIVE section (does NOT amend any LOCKED MUST); covers WritePolicy interpretation, schema, FTS compliance, `list_orphans()` rationale, `apply_staging()` DreamRunner caveat, `implementation_id`, and Tier B export caveats. **Tests:** `tests/test_memory_postgres_backend.py` (93 tests, gated by `requires_postgres` skip marker); DB-gated tests skip locally and run in CI's Postgres service matrix. pgvector semantic recall + EmbeddingBackend Protocol (200) deferred to PR 2/PR 3. **`/ship` review army (4 Sonnet specialists + Red Team + cross-family Codex, on top of the arc's 5 rounds) caught FIVE CRITICAL/P1 correctness issues the arc rounds missed — all fixed inline:** (1) idle-in-transaction — read closures ran SELECTs under `autocommit=False` without `commit()`, leaking a perpetual open transaction per recall (dual-confirmed by Codex + the perf specialist against the working LogBackend); now every read commits; (2) `_discard_conn` never removed closed connections from `_all_conns`; (3) `apply_staging` was not retry-idempotent (cleanup failure after the committed swap → re-apply → duplicate version rows) — now `_applied`-guarded; (4) `_ensure_schema` seeded v2 meta before inspecting tables → a v1 DB with tables-but-no-meta skipped the v1→v2 migration — now inspects `information_schema.columns` first; (5) URL credentials were not percent-decoded (`p%40ss` used literally) — now `unquote()`'d. Plus: `merge_into` was omitted from the Tier-B export frontmatter (broke field-losslessness — now rendered), staging table names are regex-validated at every interpolation site (SQL-injection hardening), `search()` was rewritten to a CTE computing the tsvector once (was twice), doctor output redacts the DSN, a `__repr__` scrubs the password, and `_generate_index_summary` uses FilesystemBackend's fixed section order (backend-swap parity). A round-2 Codex pass then caught one fix-introduced regression (the Case-3 return NoteRef built from a narrow SELECT dropped `pinned`/`confidence`/`captured`/`archived`/`superseded_by` — now built via `_row_to_note_ref` from the full row). 23 regression tests added (idle-txn commit ×13 parametrized, staging idempotency, percent-decode, `merge_into` round-trip, partial-DB migration, `restore_version`/`read_version`/`resolve_version_token`/`list_stale`/`list_by_type` coverage, Case-3 metadata preservation) + two false-greens fixed (an OR-assertion export test, a vacuous `hasattr` guard). Deferred perf/polish (composite indexes, `export()` N+1, `Jsonb()` adapter, `list_stale` ordering, export scope path) filed as [532](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/532). **Then CI's Postgres service caught a 6th real bug the local skip hid** (the `feedback_db_gated_tests_skip_locally` lesson): `_versions_params_from_note_row` copied a live row's JSONB columns (`sources`/`tags`/`extra_frontmatter`, read back as Python dict/list) straight into the snapshot INSERT params → `psycopg.ProgrammingError: cannot adapt type 'dict'` on EVERY version-snapshot path (merge/restore/orphan-recovery); fixed by re-serializing those columns with `json.dumps`, now caught locally by two non-DB regression tests. A CodeQL false-positive (`py/incomplete-url-substring-sanitization` on a `_redact_dsn` test's host-substring assertion) was killed by switching those assertions to exact-equality.
