Pure computation (legal transition checks, cycle detection, `evaluate_completion`, `next_sub_goal`, `progress_report`) stays in `GoalManager` above the Protocol. `read_schema_version()` is a diagnostic-only probe (returns `int | None` from `goal.md` frontmatter) — goal.md is explicitly NOT registered as a `MigratableUnit`; the spec/03 `unit_type` is locked to `Literal["memory","wiki"]` and goal schema evolution is backend-internal. `GoalExport` is a composite `ExportableResult` carrying `(goal_md_bytes, history_records_with_bytes, archived_goals_with_bytes)`; `FilesystemGoalBackend` implements `Exportable` with `supports_canonical_export=True` and is registered in the spec/40 export conformance harness. Operator override surface (partial this PR): `ATOMIC_AGENTS_GOAL_BACKEND` env var + `get_default_goal_backend(scope)` factory + `doctor.check_goal_backend()` uses the dual-probe pattern (`list_archived` + `load_goal`) per `feedback_doctor_dual_probe_pattern`. The `AtomicAgent(goal_backend=...)` constructor kwarg + `self.goal_backend` attribute are deferred to the runtime-wiring follow-up ([448](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/448)). Goal-outcome composition (`dispatch_as_outcome` coordinator wiring GoalBackend + OutcomeRunner with a pre-dispatch cost gate) was built in round 1 but removed after verification: the implementation was dead (no callers), untested, and FAIL-OPEN (unpacked `CostCheckResult` as a 2-tuple, causing `TypeError` silently swallowed as `allowed=True`, a Principle #4 cost-gate escape); maintainer ratified the defer and filed [448](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/448). `dispatch_as_outcome()` DOES ship with a correctness fix: it now persists `goal.md` before its `goal_history.jsonl` audit line (spec/41 MUST 6 ordering), with a dedicated regression test in `tests/test_goal_dispatch_audit_ordering.py`. 9 normative MUSTs in spec/41 Implementer Contract (charset validation, side-effect-free construction, capability honesty, atomic `apply_transition`, no-data-loss archive, collision-safe archive identity, idempotent archive retry, spec/41 MUST 8 stable `backend_id`, MUST 6 goal.md-before-history-line audit ordering). Conformance suite: `tests/test_goal_backend_conformance.py` (all 9 MUSTs, 887-line parametrized suite) + `tests/test_goal_filesystem.py` (filesystem-specific) + `tests/test_goal_doctor.py` (dual-probe, PASS/FAIL/SKIP ladder) + `tests/test_goal_dispatch_audit_ordering.py` (MUST 6 regression). The 4 pre-existing goal test files (`test_goal.py`, `test_agent_goal_loading.py`, `test_dashboard_goals.py`, `test_goal_outcome_composition.py`) kept intact as the zero-behavior-change regression guard. GoalBackend also registered in the spec/40 export conformance harness (`tests/test_export_protocol_conformance.py` + `tests/test_export_capability_advertisement.py`). DRAFT spec/41 ships at 267 lines; spec/12 §"Goal and sub-goal schema" cross-reference addendum added; spec/27 `check_goal_backend()` section added. **Pre-merge hardening (arc adversarial rounds + `/ship` review-army + cross-model adversarial — Claude + Codex):** `apply_transition()`'s `fields` channel is an explicit allow-set (`SUB_GOAL_TRANSITION_FIELDS`) so it can never rewrite sub-goal identity (`id`/`label`) or status; `_write_goal()` (the single serializer both `save_goal` + `apply_transition` use) now runs `validate_goal()` BEFORE the durable write — spec/41 MUST 5 write/read-validation symmetry — closing a triple-confirmed P0 where `fields={"blocked_by": "<unknown-id>"}` could persist a `goal.md` that `load_goal()` then rejects, locking the agent out of its own goal; the audit line is serialized before the goal.md write (fail-closed on non-JSON input, no partial commit, MUST 6 ordering preserved); `goal/_redact_for_error_message` gained the schemeless-DSN heuristic the other five backends already had (credential-echo parity); `doctor.check_goal_backend` treats a `goal.md` that vanished mid-check (concurrent archive) as PASS not a spurious FAIL; the `from atomic_agents.goal import main` back-compat path was restored. Follow-ups filed: [#448](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/448) (coordinator + runtime wiring), [#449](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/449) (forward-only `blocked_by` two-pass validation), [#450](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/450) (dispatch-ordering test flake harden), [#451](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/451) (`GoalExportQuery` bounded export), [#452](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/452) (archive idempotency vs no-active-goal semantics). Conformance index 53 cases. Test suite: 3,981 passed / 72 skipped → **4,075 collected** (4,003 passed / 72 skipped, +94 net, 2026-06-11).

BREAKING

- **Migration script contract changed (issue 429 — T13 refactor).** The old path-shaped `MigrationScript` Protocol (`applies_to(path: Path)` / `migrate(path: Path, dry_run: bool)`) is **removed**. Operator-authored migration scripts must be rewritten to the new per-unit handle contract: `applies_to(unit: MigratableUnit) -> bool` / `migrate(unit: MigratableUnit) -> dict` (no `dry_run` parameter — the handle enforces the gate). The old free-function API (`run_migration()`, `build_migration_plan()`, `create_snapshot()`, `restore_snapshot()`, `find_content_files()`, `get_current_vault_version()`, `discover_scripts()`) is removed from `atomic_agents.migrate`. New API: `from atomic_agents.migration import FilesystemMigrationBackend, MigratableUnit` — construct `FilesystemMigrationBackend(agents_root)` and call `backend.run_migration(target_version, dry_run)`. The `python -m atomic_agents.migrate` CLI entrypoint is **unchanged**. Migration upgrade path table: `docs/spec/03-file-formats.md §Migration upgrade path`. Rationale: backend-neutral Protocol so future DB backends satisfy the same contract without forking the runner (TENSIONS T13).

Added

- **`IdempotencyBackend` Protocol + `FilesystemDedupLedger` reference impl — DRAFT spec/45 (issue [520](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/520) PR 1 of 2, SCAFFOLDING-ONLY).** New `atomic_agents/idempotency/` subpackage: `IdempotencyBackend` (`runtime_checkable` Protocol), `DedupDecision` (frozen value-object — `is_duplicate: bool`, `state: str` (`fresh`/`in_flight`/`completed`), `prior_run_id: str | None`, `prior_result_ref: str | None`), `IdempotencyCapabilities` (`single_host_only`, `atomic_claim`, `supports_ttl`, `stores_full_result`, `supports_canonical_export`). Protocol surface: `begin(key, run_id) -> DedupDecision` (single atomic check-reserve-or-report — FRESH, IN_FLIGHT, and COMPLETED all returned as `DedupDecision` fields, NEVER raised as exceptions; only unrecoverable I/O raises `IdempotencyBackendError`), `commit(key, result_ref) -> None` (MARKER-ONLY terminal entry: `key + prior_run_id + result_ref + terminal: true`; no result body stored at any scale), `lookup(key) -> DedupDecision` (read-only, no side effects). **`FilesystemDedupLedger`:** agent-scoped (`<agent_root>/idempotency/`). `begin()` atomicity via `os.open(O_WRONLY|O_CREAT|O_EXCL)` on the lease file — exactly one concurrent `open()` succeeds; the loser gets `FileExistsError` (EEXIST) and returns IN_FLIGHT with no TOCTOU window. `commit()` writes MARKER-ONLY via `_io.atomic_write` (temp + fsync + rename). Canonical-path containment applied at every ledger read/write/claim sink: regular-file invariant + root containment + symlink-leaf + symlinked-parent rejection (the same `_require_canonical_source`-style perimeter as QueueBackend). `supports_ttl=False` (TTL sweep deferred to a follow-up PR; not enforced on `begin()`). `stores_full_result=False` (MARKER-ONLY invariant). **Doctor:** `check_idempotency_backend()` — coherence probe + DUAL-PROBE (write temp terminal entry, read back, claim-and-release a temp key) per `feedback_doctor_dual_probe_pattern`; `single_host_only` WARN (not FAIL) on `ATOMIC_AGENTS_MULTI_HOST=true`, mirroring `check_queue_backend`. **Canonical export:** `supports_canonical_export=True`; `export()` emits TERMINAL entries only, structurally EXCLUDES in-flight leases (same ephemeral-exclusion precedent as LockExport + QueueExport). `IdempotencyExport` registered in the spec/40 export conformance harness. **Operator override:** `ATOMIC_AGENTS_IDEMPOTENCY_BACKEND` env var OR constructor kwarg (NO markdown config). **DRAFT spec/45** (spec/45-idempotency-backend.md) carries the full Implementer Contract with normative MUSTs (capability advertisement, marker-only terminal-entry, lease/in-flight, atomic `begin()`, canonical-export terminal-only). TTL is an advertised capability axis (`supports_ttl=False`), not a normative MUST — the sweep is deferred. **Scope fence (PR 1 only):** NO `agent.call()` wiring, NO `idempotency_key` parameter on `call()`, NO trigger wiring (serve/queue/cron), NO `RunRecord`/JSONL audit-shape change, NO spec/22 addendum, NO spec LOCK — all of that is PR 2. Conformance suite: `tests/test_idempotency_backend_conformance.py` (~25 conformance tests, all Implementer Contract MUSTs) + `tests/test_idempotency_filesystem.py` (~10 filesystem-specific tests: O_EXCL race, MARKER-ONLY invariant, symlink containment, TTL sweep, export ephemeral exclusion). **`/ship` review hardening (5-reviewer army, all negative-control-verified):** `commit()` is now FIRST-COMMIT-WINS idempotent — a re-fired `commit()` (the at-least-once retry shape PR 2 must tame) is a no-op that preserves the original terminal's `prior_run_id` instead of overwriting it with `None` (the first commit unlinks the lease) and severing the audit link (Principle 5); `_validate_key()` additionally rejects NUL/C0 control characters; added regression coverage for the `prior_run_id` audit-link carry on IN_FLIGHT/COMPLETED and for multi-entry canonical-export sort-order; `doctor.check_idempotency_backend()` cleanup now `rmdir`s the empty probe `idempotency/` directory (a read-only diagnostic no longer leaves durable layout); `Raises:` docstrings + spec/45 surface sections corrected to note invalid keys raise `PathTraversalError` before any I/O (Principle 13). Closes #520 PR 1.

- **`MigrationBackend` Protocol + `FilesystemMigrationBackend` reference impl — spec/03 §Schema-migration re-LOCKED (issue 429, T13 refactor).** New `atomic_agents/migration/` subpackage: `MigrationBackend` (`runtime_checkable` Protocol), `MigratableUnit` (per-unit handle with `unit_type` discriminator + `dry_run`-baked-in `write_frontmatter()` no-op gate), `MigrationSnapshotRef` (opaque snapshot handle), `MigrationCapabilities`, `MigrationPlan`, `MigrationResult`, `MigrationEvent` (JSONL audit record). `FilesystemMigrationBackend` wraps today's vault-walk and tar+swap logic behind the Protocol: `enumerate_units()` (full walk, dedup by resolved path, `unit_type` set from directory), `read_schema_version()` (min across ALL units — no 50-file sample cap), `apply_unit()` (calls `applies_to(unit)` / `migrate(unit)`, verifies schema_version bump post-apply), `snapshot(target_version)` (atomic temp-then-rename tar+gz; filename embeds the **target** version), `restore(ref)` (atomic staging swap, preserves snapshots + audit log), `run_migration()` (pre-flight fail-close → exclusive lock → plan → snapshot → apply → validate → unlock → emit audit event). `MigrationRollbackUnavailable` raised pre-snapshot when `supports_transactional_rollback=False` on a real (non-dry-run) run. Vault-level exclusive lock via `FilesystemLockBackend` prevents concurrent migration runs. `MigrationEvent` JSONL appended to `<agents_root>/_migrations/migration.jsonl` on **every** exit — success, validation rollback, mid-apply exception, OR early pre-plan refusal (no-rollback fail-close, broken/absent script chain, target-below-current); pre-plan failures (those raised before the plan is built) record sentinel `from_version=-1` + the error string. A lock-busy refusal happens *after* the plan is built, so it records the resolved `from_version`, not the sentinel (spec/03 MUST 8). The audit emit is isolated from lock-release so an abnormal unlock never suppresses it; the JSONL line is written through `_io.atomic_append_jsonl` (flush + `os.fsync`), matching LogBackend's durable-append discipline so the forensic record of a destructive op survives an OS-level crash. Snapshot path emitted to STDOUT before any writes; the snapshot filename embeds the **target** version (`..._pre_v{target}_migration.tar.gz`, e.g. `pre_v2` for a run to v2), matching the spec/03 recovery examples. Post-migration validation covers **every enumerable unit** (not just the touched subset) — a script that skips a unit leaves it at the old version, and the half-migrated vault that produces is caught and rolled back (no-half-migrated-vault guarantee). 8 normative MUSTs in spec/03 §Schema-migration Implementer Contract (MUST 1: `read_schema_version()` vault-wide read-only; MUST 2: `enumerate_units()` dedup + `unit_type`; MUST 3: atomic writes; MUST 4: `snapshot(target_version)`/`restore()` protocol methods; MUST 5: fail-close on no-rollback; MUST 6: stable `backend_id`; MUST 7: version-bump post-apply verification; MUST 8: audit event on every exit, isolated from lock-release). `atomic_agents/migrate.py` updated to construct `FilesystemMigrationBackend` internally; `python -m atomic_agents.migrate` CLI entrypoint and all flags unchanged. The operator-facing `--rollback` CLI path is also audited: a manual rollback records its own `MigrationEvent` (`rolled_back=True` on success, `rolled_back=False` + the error on failure) so a forensic recovery action stays queryable (MUST 8 covers `run_migration()` OR `restore_and_audit()`). `snapshot()` **fail-closes** on a vault whose unit dir is a symlink with an absolute/escaping target — such a vault captures an unrestorable link member, so snapshot refuses at creation time (pre-mutation, naming the member + remediation) rather than handing the operator a false safety net that only fails at rollback (MUST 4 recoverable-checkpoint + MUST 5 fail-close); `restore()` wraps any unsafe-member `tarfile` rejection in `AtomicAgentsError` so it honors its documented exception contract. Symlink-dereference support for such vaults is tracked as follow-up [444](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/444). 83 conformance tests in `tests/test_migrate.py` (82 pass + 1 capability-gated skip): Protocol surface (isinstance, backend_id, capabilities), `read_schema_version` (empty/mixed/full-walk-no-sampling), `enumerate_units` (count, dedup/symlink, unit_type, cascaded layout), `apply_unit` (dry-run noop, real bump, missing-bump raises, **`migrate()`→`{}` stays falsy/skip-counted on dry-run, falsy-migrate-after-`applies_to`→`True` raises on real run + rolls the vault back**, **operator-script exceptions in `applies_to()`/`migrate()` wrapped in `AtomicAgentsError` naming script+unit with the original chained via `__cause__`**), `snapshot`/`restore` (round-trip, corrupt-tar atomic safety, mid-extract atomic safety, unknown-id raises, fresh-instance, target-version filename, **snapshot fail-close on escaping-symlink vault leaves no artifact**, **restore wraps unsafe-member tar rejection in `AtomicAgentsError`**), `restore_and_audit` (manual-rollback audit on success/failure, **pre-restore read failure degrades `from_version` to the -1 sentinel + still restores**, post-restore read failure degrades `to_version` to the sentinel + still audits), fail-close path (no-rollback stub isinstance, real-run raises, dry-run proceeds, fail-before-apply), `run_migration` integration (dry-run no-change + audit, **happy-path real run completing with `validation_passed=True`/`rolled_back=False` via monkeypatched `CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION`**, rollback-on-validation-failure, **skipped-unit validation triggers rollback**, **audit written on pre-plan failures**, **audit survives lock-release failure**, **lock-busy refusal records the resolved `from_version`, not the -1 sentinel (MUST 8)**, wiki-schema dispatch, target-below-current, no-scripts, audit-real-run), custom-frontmatter-field preservation, capability-gated conformance matrix (filesystem runs; no-rollback stub SKIPS), breaking-signature assertions, **CLI dispatch smoke (`main()` routes `--status` / `--list-snapshots` / no-arg-help through a freshly constructed backend with correct exit codes — the operator entrypoint preserved across the refactor)**. The standard validators reject any `schema_version != CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION`, so a real cross-version migration completes end-to-end only once the package bumps that constant (documented in spec/03 §Validation); until then a real migration to a not-yet-adopted target validates-and-rolls-back by design. Legacy (pre-`schema_version`) v0→v1 support tracked as follow-up [#439](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/439).

- **`Exportable` companion Protocol + canonical-shape export contract — spec/40 (LOCKED)** ([379](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/379) — PR 1 of 1). Delivers the T15 Position B spine: the canonical file shape is always reconstructable as a tested round-trip contract. New `atomic_agents/export/` package: `Exportable` (`runtime_checkable` Protocol — NOT bolted onto the 13 locked backends, structural composition only), typed `ExportableResult` hierarchy (`MemoryExport`, `LogExport`, `MandateExport`, `CorpusExport`, `LockExport`, `SecretExport`), per-backend query types (`MemoryExportQuery`, `LogExportQuery`, `MandateExportQuery`, `CorpusExportQuery`, `LockExportQuery`, `SecretExportQuery`), `SecretExportRef` (logical-key + hint, NEVER plaintext), and shared per-type renderer (`render_run_record_bytes`, `render_note_bytes_from_raw`/`from_object`, `render_corpus_page_bytes_from_raw`/`from_object`, `render_mandates_md`, `render_secret_export_bytes`). Filesystem identity export impls for five state backends (`export_memory`, `export_log`, `export_mandate`, `export_corpus`, `export_lock`) plus `export_secret` in `atomic_agents/export/filesystem.py`. `supports_canonical_export: bool = False` (LAST field, backward-compatible default) added to `LogCapabilities`, `LockCapabilities`, `MandateCapabilities`, `CorpusCapabilities`, `SecretCapabilities`, and `MCPServerRegistryCapabilities`; `FilesystemMemoryBackend` uses a `property` (matching `supports_semantic_search` shape, MemoryCapabilities dataclass convergence deferred to [#431](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/431)). All six PR1 filesystem impls advertise `True`. 9 normative MUSTs in spec/40 Implementer Contract: Protocol composition, capability-claim honesty, `export_all()` thin wrapper, Tier A byte-exact fidelity, UTF-8/LF/no-BOM, state-enumeration-not-retrieval, snapshot-consistency bound, RunRecord key-order (`to_dict()` insertion order NOT `canonical_json()` sorted), and SecretBackend never-leak invariant (ABSOLUTE, unconditional). `MandateExport` includes `meta_by_scope` for the project-root `# _meta` policy block (security boundary: dropping it silently reverts `forbidden` → `open`); `render_mandates_md(mandates, meta=None)` emits and re-parses the block losslessly. `LockExport.lock_file_names` is always `[]` (ephemeral runtime state, never migrated). The full `MandateConstraints` surface round-trips byte-exactly — all USD caps, `allowed_targets`/`blocked_targets` (with custom kinds), `time_window` (seconds preserved), `action_class`, `unconstrained` + justification, and revocation fields. `export_log` returns every matching line verbatim for `export_all()` (unbounded); a date-bounded query prefilters shards for performance, with the verbatim caveat for hand-misfiled lines documented in spec/40. 91 new tests: `tests/test_export_capability_advertisement.py` (capability advertisement, not capability-gated — prevents skip-all-by-accident) and `tests/test_export_protocol_conformance.py` (round-trip conformance with one shared `assert_canonical_roundtrip(backend, write_fn, expected_bytes_fn)` helper, capability-gated skips, per-backend sections). Cross-spec addenda shipped in spec/20 (MemoryBackend), spec/21 (LockBackend), spec/22 (LogBackend), spec/29 (MandateBackend), spec/34 (CorpusBackend), spec/38 (SecretBackend). Per-backend SQLite/Postgres/Redis/GCP/HTTP-registry export impls deferred to later PRs. `atomic-agents export` CLI deferred to [430](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/430). `include_versions` history scan deferred to [#433](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/433). Custom operator keys for SecretBackend export deferred to [#432](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/432). Shard-window prefiltering for bounded LogExport disk I/O deferred to [#434](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/434). Tier B object-graph renderers (no callers until structured backends land) and export error-path edges deferred to [#435](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/435). spec/40 is **LOCKED** on filesystem proof (contract-first ruling); CLAUDE.md Principle #1 and TENSIONS.md T15 now cross-reference it as the implementation contract.

- **`IdempotencyBackend` agent.call() wiring — spec/45 LOCKED (issue [520](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/520) PR 2 of 2, ARC-CLOSER).** Wires the PR1 `FilesystemDedupLedger` into `agent.call()` via a two-phase deduplication gate, closes spec/45 as LOCKED, and adds spec/22 versioned normative addendum. **Two-phase gate (reconciled order):** run_id reset → OTel span open → `lookup()` BEFORE lock acquire → lock acquire → `_check_cost_guardrails()` → `begin()` AFTER cost gate. Phase 1 (lookup before lock): a COMPLETED decision short-circuits immediately — no LLM call, no lock — by writing a `status='deduped'` JSONL audit record and returning `Response.deduped_response()`. Phase 2 (begin after cost gate): `begin()` returns one of THREE states and all are handled — a COMPLETED decision (the realistic lookup→commit→begin race, where a concurrent twin committed the key after our Phase-1 lookup saw FRESH) short-circuits identically to Phase 1 (deduped record + `Response.deduped_response()`, NO LLM run, NO lease claimed) per spec/45 W7; an IN_FLIGHT decision writes a `status='in_flight'` JSONL record and raises `DedupInFlight`; a FRESH decision sets `_idempotency_lease_held=True` and proceeds. (Without the W7 COMPLETED branch the race silently double-spends the LLM call — the dedup's core guarantee.) **Lifecycle invariants:** `commit()` is called AFTER `self._log(log_record)` on the success path (Principle 5 — durable JSONL first, then ledger terminal); `release_lease()` runs in `try/finally` when `_idempotency_lease_held=True` (catches crashes so the key doesn't wedge permanently); `IdempotencyBackendError` from `release_lease()` is swallowed in finally (must not interrupt lock release). **new `idempotency_key: str | None = None` keyword-only parameter on `agent.call()`** (zero-change for all callers that don't use dedup). **Body-hash opt-in** (`dedup_body_hash_enabled` in `AgentConfig` + model.md `# Dedup Body Hash` section): when enabled and no explicit key is supplied, derives an implicit key as `sha256(work_item+model+max_tokens+temperature)` — prevents duplicate LLM spend for bit-identical re-deliveries without requiring caller key management. **`RunRecord` audit fields (spec/22 versioned normative addendum):** `idempotency_key: str | None` + `replayed_run_id: str | None` added to `RunRecord` dataclass; `to_dict()` uses the None-omit pattern; `from_dict()` decodes both fields; `LogQuery.idempotency_key: str | None` AND-predicate filter added to ALL THREE reference log backends — FilesystemLogBackend (`_matches` predicate), SQLiteLogBackend (WHERE clause + `idx_idempotency_key`), and PostgresLogBackend (WHERE clause + `idx_idempotency_key`) — and pinned in the canonical parametrized cross-backend conformance suite (`test_log_protocol_conformance.py::test_query_filters_by_idempotency_key` + `::test_query_idempotency_key_round_trips_replayed_run_id`, mirroring the `mandate_id` precedent) so a third-party LogBackend that omits the predicate FAILS the documented gate rather than passing it (Design Principle 2/10). `cost_usd` MUST be ABSENT (not `0.0`) from `status='deduped'` and `status='in_flight'` JSONL records. **SQLite + Postgres v1→v2 schema migration** (ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN idempotency_key TEXT + replayed_run_id TEXT + idx_idempotency_key index on both): runs automatically on first open under the per-backend cold-start serialization (SQLite meta-row check / Postgres advisory-xact-lock); migration code runs BEFORE index creation so the new column exists before the new index is built. The SQLite migration guards each `ADD COLUMN` with a `PRAGMA table_info()` existence check so a process that crashes mid-migration (DDL implicitly commits in `sqlite3`, leaving one column added but `schema_version` still 1) resumes cleanly on reopen instead of wedging on `duplicate column name` — parity with Postgres's `ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS`. (PostgresLogBackend `_INSERT_COLUMNS` + insert tuple + `_row_to_record` updated in lockstep so the two audit fields are persisted and read back, not silently dropped.) **Serve layer (spec/45 PR2 trigger wiring):** `make_app()` gains `idempotency_header: str = 'Idempotency-Key'` kwarg; `ServeConfig` gains `idempotency_header` field (serve.md ` Idempotency Header` section + `ATOMIC_AGENTS_SERVE_IDEMPOTENCY_HEADER` env override); `call_agent` handler extracts the header (rejects empty/`.`/`..`/path-separators/C0-control-chars with HTTP 422 — the guard mirrors the backend `_validate_key` so a client-controlled invalid key surfaces as a clean 422 instead of reaching the backend and mapping to a misleading 500; an over-length key is REJECTED with HTTP 422 — NOT silently truncated — at the backend's `_MAX_KEY_LEN` (2048), because an idempotency key is a correctness-bearing identifier whose silent narrowing would alias two distinct caller keys into one dedup bucket and serve a new request an unrelated cached result); a deduped `Response` maps to HTTP 200 `{status:'deduped', served_from_cache:true, replayed_run_id, run_id}` plus `result_ref` only when the ledger carries one (absent when `None`, matching the None-omit convention) — the cached output is NOT inlined (the ledger is MARKER-ONLY per spec/45 MUST 5; the caller resolves the bytes via `result_ref`/OutcomeBackend, so no misleading empty `output` field is emitted); `DedupInFlightWithRunId` maps to HTTP 409 `{status:'in_flight', prior_run_id, run_id}`. **Queue trigger wiring (spec/45 W8):** `extract_queue_idempotency_key(payload: dict) -> str | None` helper added to `_cascade.py` — reads ONLY `payload['idempotency_key']` for cascade cron dispatch; absent/empty/non-string → `None` (no dedup). Deliberately does NOT fall back to `payload['id']`: queue ids are not guaranteed globally unique across distinct work items, so an id fallback could false-dedup two unrelated runs (silently dropping a real run). The trigger surface fails OPEN (runs the LLM) rather than risk a dropped run — normative as W8 with a no-`id`-fallback negative-control test. **Cron trigger wiring:** `cron_tick_key(agent_name, schedule_name, when, granularity) -> str` added to `atomic_agents.idempotency` (spec/45 MUST 14): floors `when` to the granularity bucket (minute/hour/day/week), timezone-invariant via UTC conversion, format `<agent>:<sched>:<bucket_epoch>`; a NAIVE `when` raises `ValueError` (a naive datetime would be floored against the host's local timezone, defeating cross-host invariance). **spec/22 versioned normative addendum** (outside the 8-MUST count, following spec/09 precedent): four normative properties for `status='deduped'` and `status='in_flight'` JSONL records — `cost_usd` ABSENT, `idempotency_key` set, `replayed_run_id` set on deduped only, all three reference backend implementations updated. **spec/45 LOCKED** (Status: DRAFT → LOCKED): MUST 13 (release_lease idempotency/best-effort), MUST 14 (cron_tick_key bucket stability + tz-aware-required), W1–W8 PR2 wiring contract added (W7 = the Phase-2 begin()→COMPLETED race short-circuit; W8 = the queue-trigger extractor's no-`id`-fallback rule); PR2 scope section updated from deferred to shipped; `release_lease()` Protocol method documented in surface section; `supports_ttl=False` corrected (TTL sweep deferred). **docs updates:** `protocols-shipped.md` corrected (PR status DRAFT→LOCKED, two-phase-gate W7 + queue-extractor W8 added, test counts refreshed); `docs/spec/37-serve.md` gains normative Deduped (HTTP 200) + In-flight (HTTP 409) response paragraphs; `docs/spec/01-anatomy.md` model.md example gains the ` Dedup Body Hash` section; `docs/implementation/cron-agent.md` ` Deduplication` section documents `cron_tick_key` (tz-aware), `extract_queue_idempotency_key` (no-`id`-fallback), and implicit body-hash dedup via model.md. **Tests:** `tests/test_idempotency_pr2_wiring.py` (69 tests) — RunRecord round-trip, LogQuery filter (filesystem + SQLite), SQLite v1→v2 migration, cron_tick_key bucketing, extract_queue_idempotency_key (incl. a W8 no-`id`-fallback negative-control: an `id`-only payload returns `None`, and an explicit key wins over a present `id`), model.md Dedup Body Hash parser (incl. anchored-h2 negative controls: an ` Dedup Body Hash` h3 and a longer ` Dedup Body Hash Strategy` h2 must NOT enable dedup), Response.deduped_response() fields, serve HTTP 200 deduped (result_ref present, no `output`) / HTTP 409 in_flight, header validation (incl. over-length-key → 422 + an at-cap boundary key reaching the runner untruncated as the negative control), body-hash key derived-in-call (spy-captured, not recomputed) + a format-agnostic stability/discrimination oracle (immune to lockstep template edits) + explicit-key-wins, AND a full `agent.call()` two-phase-gate integration suite (W1 Phase-1 COMPLETED, W7 Phase-2 begin→COMPLETED race, W3 IN_FLIGHT raise + record, W4 commit-after-log ordering, W5 release-on-exception incl. BaseException crash, W6 idempotency_key on every keyed ok run) — each with a per-invariant negative control verified RED by stripping the corresponding guard. `tests/test_idempotency_backend_conformance.py` (68 tests, +release_lease MUST 13) + `tests/test_idempotency_filesystem.py` (44 tests, +release_lease symlink-leaf refusal + EACCES mapping) + Postgres parity tests (schema columns + index + idempotency_key WHERE-clause static checks AND a behavioral mock-conn v1→v2 migration-ladder-ordering test asserting both `ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS` ALTERs + the meta `UPDATE` run BEFORE the `idx_idempotency_key` `CREATE INDEX`, plus a v2-DB-skips-migration negative control) in `tests/test_log_postgres_backend.py`. SQLite migration coverage upgraded from static to behavioral: a real on-disk v1 DB is opened and asserted to migrate to v2 (columns + index + version bump + legacy-row-survives-NULL), plus a partial-crash-resume test (one column already present, meta still v1 → reopen completes without `duplicate column name`). New `tests/test_source_hygiene.py` AST guard rejects any `if False`/`False and`/`and False`/`if True and` dead/strip boolean guard in the runtime package (regression guard for the un-restored negative-control strip). `tests/test_otel_tracing.py` gains a Phase-1 span-leak guard (a non-conforming idempotency backend that raises an un-wrapped exception from `lookup()` MUST still finalize, not leak, the call span — the Phase-1 lookup gate now mirrors the lock-acquire block's broad `except BaseException` span finalization) PLUS two outcome-attribute assertions for the new short-circuit spans: `begin()→IN_FLIGHT` finalizes the call span with `outcome=in_flight` (negative control: stripping the pre-raise `_finalize_call_span` regresses it to `outcome=error`) and Phase-1 `lookup()→COMPLETED` finalizes with `outcome=deduped`. The call()-integration wiring tests inject in-memory fake Lock + Log backends (the dedup decision path is the behavior under test; real-filesystem lock/log timing is the documented macOS APFS WAL flake surface and is removed from these tests). **`/ship` review hardening (5 specialists + Red Team, fresh-Opus cross-family — Codex out of credits — all fixes negative-control-verified):** Red Team caught two CRITICAL state-machine bugs past the arc's 5 rounds: (1) a **deferred (ESCALATE) run wrongly committed the idempotency key** — a retry then returned an empty deduped `Response` that had lost `deferred`/`escalation_queue_ids`, silently dropping the escalation; the success-path `commit()` is now guarded `and not _response_deferred`, so a deferred run releases the lease and a retry re-runs and re-surfaces the escalation (spec/45 W4 clause added); (2) **body-hash auto-derivation leaked into framework-internal calls** (eval repeat-invocations, delegate children) — identical inputs returned `text=''` deduped results, breaking eval scoring; auto-derivation is now gated to redelivery-capable external triggers (`http`/`queue`/`cron`) via `_BODY_HASH_AUTO_DERIVE_TRIGGERS` (an explicit key is still honored on any trigger; spec/45 + model.md clause added). Also: the W6 cost-skip records (pre-loop + mid-loop) now tag `idempotency_key` on keyed runs; `cron_tick_key` rejects `:`/path-separators/control chars in `agent_name`/`schedule_name` (false-dedup collision); `idx_idempotency_key` is now a PARTIAL index (`WHERE idempotency_key IS NOT NULL`) on SQLite + Postgres so the common no-dedup insert pays no index-maintenance cost; `doctor.check_idempotency_backend` dual-probe extended to `release_lease` (MUST 13, per `feedback_doctor_dual_probe_pattern`); the `_serve_completed_dedup` closure is built only on the keyed path (zero-overhead `None` default). Test suite: 4802 tests collected / 4730 passed / 72 skipped / 0 failures (2026-06-17).

1.1.0

Security-fix + GCP-delivery release. Closes three HIGH advisories (GHSA-xhcr-cqfr-m3hv, GHSA-rm43-82j9-r4mj, GHSA-j659-8xh6-5pq5) and the public hardening issues 400-406, ships the GCP Secret Manager backend (340 PR 2, spec/38 LOCKED) + the corrected GCP v0 deployment topology (395), and bumps dependency floors. Upgrade from 1.0.0 is recommended for all deployments; the three HIGH fixes default-on with no config change.

Security

- **Cleartext-HTTP MCP catalog → MITM → local RCE hardened (GHSA-xhcr-cqfr-m3hv)** — two new defense-in-depth gates close the path where a network man-in-the-middle on a cleartext `http://` MCP catalog injects an arbitrary `command` field into an `MCPServerSpec`, which `MCPClientPool` then spawns as a subprocess. (1) **Transport scheme gate (spec/36 MUST 11):** `HTTPMCPServerRegistryBackend` now refuses cleartext `http://` for non-loopback catalog hosts unless the operator explicitly opts in via `ATOMIC_AGENTS_MCP_SERVER_REGISTRY_ALLOW_HTTP=1` (only the literal `"1"` counts). Loopback hosts (`127.0.0.0/8`, `::1`, literal `localhost`) are always allowed — loopback traffic cannot be intercepted by a network adversary. The loopback check uses `urlparse().hostname` (not `.netloc`) to resist userinfo-injection (`http://127.0.0.1evil.com/`); the opt-in flag is resolved once at construction (no TOCTOU); a URL too malformed to classify fails closed. The gate fires on every I/O method — `list_mcp_servers`, `load_mcp_server`, `load_all_mcp_servers`, `validate`, **and the write paths `install`/`uninstall`** — before `_ensure_probed()`, so a refused scheme blocks even when the capability cache is warm. (2) **Spawn gate / command-basename allowlist (spec/36 MUST 12):** `MCPClientPool` validates every spec's `command` basename against an allowlist before constructing `StdioServerParameters` — no subprocess is spawned for a disallowed command. Default set `{npx, uvx, python, python3, node, docker}`. Operators replace (not extend) the set via a `# Allowed commands` section in the agent's `mcp.md`; an empty section is explicit deny-all (with a loud warning). `MCPCommandNotAllowed` (subclass of `MCPServerConnectFailed`) propagates out of `connect_all()` fail-closed — it is NOT soft-skipped. The allowlist is wired end-to-end on the live agent path: `agent.call()` resolves it from `self._profile.mcp_md_raw` and threads it into the pool. A spawn-gate refusal writes a JSONL run record carrying `run_id` + the error class (Principle 5 audit trail). **Honest scope limit:** the spawn gate blocks direct shell/exec-binary injection but does NOT prevent code execution through an allowlisted interpreter invoked with a code-exec flag (`python -c`, `node -e`); operators handling untrusted catalogs should narrow ` Allowed commands`. spec/36 re-locked: MUSTs 1-10 covered by the parametrized conformance suite, MUST 11 by `tests/test_mcp_server_registry_http_backend.py`, MUST 12 (a `MCPClientPool` pool-layer invariant, not a backend method) by `tests/test_mcp.py`. New tests across `tests/test_mcp.py` and `tests/test_mcp_server_registry_http_backend.py`. Default behavior unchanged for existing deployments (https and loopback http keep working; default command set covers the common MCP runners).
- **Deploy-doc hardening: perimeter identity-header stripping (405) + shared-Redis AUTH/TLS (406).** Two documentation-only hardening notes from the 2026-06-09 security review. `docs/deployment/serve.md` now states explicitly that the perimeter MUST strip or overwrite the configured identity header on every inbound request (the framework trusts it unconditionally, so a forwarded client-set header spoofs the audit-trail caller identity); a generic header name like `X-Forwarded-User` or a plain forwarding proxy is the risk. `docs/spec/21-lock-backend.md` adds a shared-Redis hardening note: require `rediss://` (TLS), AUTH credentials, and a distinct `key_prefix` per deployment when one Redis is shared, and flags the spec's `redis://localhost` examples as loopback-only illustrations. No framework-side JWT verification is added (spec/37 MUST 6 keeps perimeter auth final). No code change.
- Hygiene version floor bumps for three transitive dependencies. CVE paths were each triaged as unreachable in this framework (the vulnerable code paths are not exercised by atomic-agents-stack's use of these libraries); bumps are preventive hygiene, not emergency fixes.
- `idna>=3.15` (was 3.13; CVE-2026-45409 — internationalized domain name codec; not reached via any agent call path).
- `pyjwt>=2.13.0` (was 2.12.1; PYSEC-2026-175/177/178/179 — JWT decoding; atomic-agents-stack uses pyjwt only as a transitive dep of `mcp`, which uses it for OAuth token validation in MCP server connections, not for any user-facing JWT decode path).
- `starlette>=1.0.1` (was 0.27; PYSEC-2026-161 — Starlette request handling; the `[serve]` extra floor was raised from the old 0.27 baseline to 1.0.1 and the lock resolved to 1.2.1). Floor encoded in `pyproject.toml` `[project.optional-dependencies] serve`; `idna` and `pyjwt` encoded as `[tool.uv] constraint-dependencies` (transitive floor pins that do not add to user-visible install requirements).

Added

- **`MemoryBackend` operator override surface — `ATOMIC_AGENTS_MEMORY_BACKEND` env var + `AtomicAgent(memory_backend=...)` kwarg + `get_default_memory_backend` factory + doctor coherence check** ([382](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/382) — PR 1). Wires operator selection of the `MemoryBackend` to match the shipped `LockBackend` / `LogBackend` / `AgentProfileBackend` override surface exactly. `ATOMIC_AGENTS_MEMORY_BACKEND` env var (default `"filesystem"`) selects the backend; `AtomicAgent(..., memory_backend=...)` constructor kwarg wins when supplied. Unknown backend ids fail-fast at agent construction time (`BackendNotRegistered`) with the full known-id list — no silent filesystem fallback. New public `get_default_memory_backend(agent_root, *, lock_backend=None)` factory in `atomic_agents.memory` routes all state-writing construction sites (`agent.py`, `dream.py`, `tuning.py`, `_capture.py`, `_versioning.py`) through one selection seam; `lock_backend` is threaded through so `apply_staging` and `agent.call()` share the same lock backend instance. Uniform construction contract: every registered `MemoryBackend` MUST accept `(agent_root: Path, *, lock_backend=None)` — written into spec/20 as a normative MUST and enforced by a registry conformance test. Delegate threading deliberately absent: memory is per-agent state (each agent has its own `memory/` directory), so `AtomicAgent.delegate()` never threads the coordinator's memory backend to a child — each child resolves its own via the same process-global env selection (distinct from persona/corpus fleet-scoped config, which IS threaded). `DreamRunner` guards against non-filesystem backends at construction time and raises `NotImplementedError` rather than breaking silently at apply time (routing through a backend-agnostic staging-adopt path deferred to [#396](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/396)). Two new doctor checks mirror the `check_lock_backend` / `check_locks` pair: `check_memory_backend_config` (coherence — known id, constructs for non-filesystem) and `check_memory_backend` (liveness — factory resolves and `stats()` returns); both route through `get_default_memory_backend` (doctor-reuses-factory invariant). `ATOMIC_AGENTS_MEMORY_BACKEND_URL` companion var deferred to [#258](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/258) (the first non-filesystem memory backend that needs a connection URL). spec/20 updated: `# Operator override surface` section added + spec re-locked via the drift gate. 29 new tests in `tests/test_memory_operator_override.py` covering: factory env-var path, kwarg-wins, lock threading, registry helpers, uniform construction contract + registry conformance, doctor coherence + liveness checks including a registered-backend PASS. Default stays filesystem; zero behavior change for existing deployments.
- **`VertexGeminiLLMBackend` — fourth reference LLM backend (Gemini on Google Cloud Vertex AI)** ([345](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/345)). New `atomic_agents/llm/vertex_gemini.py` implements the `SyncLLMBackend` Protocol over the `google-genai>=1.0` SDK with `vertexai=True`. `provider_id="vertex-gemini"`; routes `vertex/gemini-*` model ids (scoped to `gemini-` so the upcoming `vertex/claude-*` backend lands without prefix collision). Registers lazily on the first model lookup behind a guarded try/except — missing `google-genai` logs at DEBUG (not WARNING, so a non-Vertex deployment sees no first-call noise) and continues. Optional `atomic-agents-stack[vertex]` extra (`google-genai`). **Auth is Application Default Credentials (ADC) only** — no API-key string; operators run `gcloud auth application-default login` or attach a service account (express-mode API-key auth deferred to [#378](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/378)). Per-family honest capability table: `tools`/`tool_results=True` for flash/pro (False for flash-lite, which lacks function calling); `cache_control=False` for all families (Vertex context caching is a separate resource API incompatible with the `CacheDirective` pattern — [#377](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/377)); `streaming=False` (deferred to `StreamingLLMBackend`); `vision=False` for all families (no image-block translation path yet — advertising True would violate conformance rule 4; [#376](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/376)); longest-prefix family resolution so dated aliases like `gemini-2.0-flash-lite-001` resolve to the correct (more specific) family. `call()` translates system prompt via `GenerateContentConfig.system_instruction` (not a message), strips JSON-Schema `title` keys (Gemini's OpenAPI subset rejects them), extracts token counts from `usage_metadata.prompt_token_count`/`candidates_token_count`, and **fails loud (raises `AtomicAgentsError`) when a content-producing response omits usage_metadata** rather than reporting 0 tokens and silently disarming the cost guardrails (`usage_reporting=True` honesty). `format_tool_results()` emits the two-turn continuation (model-role `function_call` echo + user-role `function_response`) Vertex requires — a `function_response` not immediately preceded by its matching `function_call` is rejected `400 INVALID_ARGUMENT`; every result is transmitted (orphan results are not silently dropped). `count_tokens()` is a conservative char/3 heuristic that accounts for both `content`- and `parts`-shaped messages so multi-iteration tool loops are not under-counted. Four `vertex/gemini-*` entries added to `_costs.PRICING` (verified against the Vertex AI pricing page). New `doctor check_vertex_credentials()` resolves ADC and mints an OAuth token via `credentials.refresh(Request())` — proves credentials are usable without a billable LLM call; WARN (not FAIL) when `GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT` is unset (Cloud Run / GKE auto-resolve it). spec/31 updated with the `VertexGeminiLLMBackend` reference description; README / `protocols-shipped.md` / explainer / CLAUDE.md updated from three to four reference backends.
- **OpenTelemetry trace export — off-by-default tracing seam + `[otel]` optional extra + DRAFT spec/39** ([341](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/341) — PR 1 of arc). Emits one `atomic_agents.call` span per `agent.call()` when `ATOMIC_AGENTS_TRACING_ENABLED=true` AND `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT` is set; otherwise installs no TracerProvider and leaves the OTel API's default `ProxyTracerProvider` in place — zero cost, zero background threads, no warnings. `opentelemetry-api>=1.20,<2.0` added to core dependencies (genuine no-op path; importing `tracing.py` costs nothing when tracing is off). SDK + OTLP/HTTP exporter (`opentelemetry-sdk`, `opentelemetry-exporter-otlp-proto-http`) are opt-in behind the new `atomic-agents-stack[otel]` extra — HTTP exporter only, no gRPC (avoids the grpcio dependency weight). New `atomic_agents/tracing.py` module: `_ensure_tracer_provider()` (installs SDK provider only when the global is still the bare `ProxyTracerProvider` — never clobbers a host provider), `get_tracer()`, locked span/attribute name constants (`SPAN_AGENT_CALL`, `ATTR_AGENT_NAME`, `ATTR_TRIGGER`, `ATTR_RUN_ID`, `ATTR_MODEL`, `ATTR_OUTCOME`, `ATTR_COST_USD`, `ATTR_INPUT_TOKENS`, `ATTR_OUTPUT_TOKENS`, `ATTR_TOOL_ITERATIONS`), and pre-declared child-span / `gen_ai.*` constants for later arc PRs. `agent.call()` span lifecycle: opened immediately after `run_id` reset (span carries the fresh `run_id`), attached as active OTel context, ended on all exit paths via idempotent finalizer — normal return, exception, lock_busy, pre-loop cost-skip, mid-loop cost-cap. Every span mutation (open-path `start_span`/`context.attach`/`set_attribute`, all `set_status`/`record_exception`/`set_attribute` / `span.end` calls in the finalizer) is wrapped so a tracing failure can never propagate, crash `call()`, or prevent lock release. Outcome precedence: `error > lock_busy > skipped > deferred > ok`. DRAFT spec/39 ships at 253 lines with 9 normative MUSTs in the Implementer Contract. 24 new tests in `tests/test_otel_tracing.py` covering: base import without `[otel]` extra; disabled/no-endpoint zero-span paths; successful call `ok` span with cost/token/iteration attributes; exception re-raise with `error` span + partial spend (MUST 1 pin); lock_busy / pre-loop cost-skip / mid-loop cost-cap outcomes; OTel context baseline after call; host-provider non-clobber (MUST 6); span-open fault tolerance (MUST 3 symmetric); OTLP exporter constructed with no `endpoint=` arg so the exporter reads env vars itself. Child spans (`gen_ai.client.chat`, tool / helper / delegate) are constants-declared but not yet emitted — deferred to later arc PR.
- **`GCPSecretManagerBackend`: second SecretBackend reference impl + `[gcp]` extra + `check_gcp_secret_backend()` + LOCKED spec/38** ([340](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/340), PR 2 of 2). New `atomic_agents/secret_backend/gcp.py` with `GCPSecretManagerBackend` + `make_gcp_secret_backend_from_url` factory. Resolves the `latest` version of each GCP Secret Manager secret live on every `get()` call (no caching, `supports_rotation=True`); key-to-secret-name mapping `key.lower().replace('_', '-')` (e.g. `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` → `anthropic-api-key`); resource path `projects/<project_id>/secrets/<name>/versions/latest`. Auth via ADC / workload identity (side-effect-free `__init__`; gRPC channel deferred to first `get()` via `_get_client()`). Thread-safe lazy client construction via double-checked locking + `threading.Lock`; idempotent `close()` releases the gRPC transport. GCP error mapping: `NotFound` → `SecretNotFound`; all other `GoogleAPICallError` → `SecretError` (fail-closed, base-class catch). Trailing newline/whitespace stripped from decoded payload (MUST 7; prevents silent auth failures from secrets created via `echo` instead of `printf '%s'`). MUST 4 secrecy: exception messages name the key and resource path only, never the resolved value. `locate()` source label: `gcp-secret-manager:projects/<id>/secrets/<name>` (built from request path, not API response). `capabilities`: `supports_rotation=True`, `supports_audit_logging=False` (GCP Cloud Audit Logs are operator-configured, not automatic; https://cloud.google.com/secret-manager/docs/audit-logging), `persists_plaintext=False` (AES-256 encrypted payloads in Google-managed KMS). New `[gcp]` optional extra (`google-cloud-secret-manager>=2.16`; lazy-imported, base startup does not load the SDK). `get_default_secret_backend()` gcp branch updated from placeholder-raise to real `make_gcp_secret_backend_from_url` construction; `ImportError` when `[gcp]` extra absent raises `SecretBackendNotRegistered` (not raw `ImportError`) naming the extra. New `check_gcp_secret_backend()` doctor function (ADC liveness probe via `credentials.refresh(Request())`, non-billable, mirrors `check_vertex_credentials()`; WARN when `GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT` absent); wired from `check_secret_backend()` when `backend_id == "gcp"`. `check_secret_backend()` fix_hint updated (removed stale "ships at PR 2" reference; added known ids `filesystem` + `gcp`). spec/38 LOCKED: status block, shipping plan, module layout tree, capability tables, operator surface table, MUST 2 scoped to filesystem with GCP addendum, GCP doctor check section, test-count claim all reconciled per LOCK ceremony. `docs/deployment/secret-backend.md` migration guide added (filesystem default as permanent first-class choice; GCP as one concrete opt-in subsection). `google-cloud-secret-manager>=2.16` added to `[dependency-groups].dev` (mirrors fakeredis/psycopg/otel-sdk precedent; `google.api_core.exceptions` must be importable for error-mapping branches). Conformance fixture extended to `params=['filesystem', 'gcp']` with `make_present` fixture; all three fixtures (`backend`, `force_absent`, `force_other_sources_absent`) have GCP branches; `monkeypatch.setenv`-only live-key injections replaced with `make_present` calls. 74 new tests (49 GCP-specific in `tests/test_secret_backend_gcp.py` + 25 new `[gcp]` parametrized conformance tests). Total secret backend test count: 149 (56 conformance [25 parametrized x 2 backends + 6 non-parametrized charset tests] + 49 GCP-specific + 11 CLI + 33 filesystem-specific). Plus 8 new doctor tests for `check_gcp_secret_backend()` + the `check_secret_backend()` gcp delegation, including a delegated-FAIL backend_id-propagation test (`tests/test_doctor_gcp_secret_backend.py`). protocols-shipped.md SecretBackend section updated from DRAFT/PR-1-of-2 to LOCKED with GCPSecretManagerBackend.
- **`SecretBackend` Protocol + `FilesystemSecretBackend` reference impl + `atomic-agents secrets` CLI + DRAFT spec/38** ([340](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/340) — PR 1 of 2, the thirteenth backend protocol). Abstracts credential resolution behind a Protocol so alternate secret substrates (GCP Secret Manager, AWS Secrets Manager, HashiCorp Vault) drop in without forking. New `atomic_agents/secret_backend/` package with `__init__.py` (registry: `register_secret_backend` / `get_secret_backend` / `list_secret_backends` + `get_default_secret_backend` factory + `_redact_for_error_message` helper), `types.py` (`SecretRef` frozen dataclass + `SecretCapabilities` frozen dataclass), `backend.py` (`runtime_checkable SecretBackend` Protocol + `SecretError` / `SecretNotFound` / `SecretBackendNotRegistered` exception hierarchy), and `filesystem.py` (`FilesystemSecretBackend` resolving env vars → macOS Keychain → `~/.config/atomic_agents/keys.json` in fixed non-configurable priority). Package name `secret_backend/` (not `secrets/`) avoids shadowing the Python stdlib `secrets` module; mirrors `mcp_registry/` naming convention. **`_get_key()` superseded**: the credential cascade now lives in `FilesystemSecretBackend`; all six live callers rewired (`doctor.py:check_provider_keys`, `init/wizard.py`, `llm/openai_compat.py`, `llm/anthropic.py`, `judge/llm.py`, `eval._provider_available`). `_get_key()` / `_get_anthropic_key()` / `_get_openai_key()` / `_get_moonshot_key()` preserved as thin redirect wrappers. `atomic-agents secrets check <KEY>` — resolves via the configured backend and prints present/absent + source label without ever printing the value (spec/38 secrecy MUSTs 4-6). `atomic-agents secrets which <KEY>` — prints the source label only (`env:ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`, `keychain:atomic-agents-anthropic`, `config:~/.config/atomic_agents/keys.json:anthropic`). `atomic-agents secrets validate` — instantiates the configured backend and prints capability snapshot. `check_secret_backend()` doctor check added (14th `check_*_backend`): PASS on `get_default_secret_backend()` + capability advertisement structurally valid; FAIL on `SecretError` or unexpected exception; SKIP when no `--agent` supplied (grouped with agent-scoped backend checks for output consistency; agent-free check is `atomic-agents secrets validate`). DRAFT spec/38 ships at 224 lines with 9 normative MUSTs in the Implementer Contract (key charset validation, machine-scoped sources, capability honesty, no value in exceptions, no value in `locate()` output, no value in CLI output, empty-string as absent, `has()` delegation, no caching). `ATOMIC_AGENTS_SECRET_BACKEND` env var (default `"filesystem"`); `ATOMIC_AGENTS_SECRET_BACKEND_URL` reserved for GCP backend (PR 2). GCP backend raises a clear "ships at PR 2" error when configured early. 75 new tests across `tests/test_secret_backend_protocol_conformance.py`, `tests/test_secret_backend_filesystem.py`, and `tests/test_secret_backend_cli.py`.
- `PostgresLogBackend` — the first Postgres reference implementation of a backend protocol, conforming to the locked `LogBackend` Protocol (spec/22). Opt-in `atomic-agents-stack[postgres]` extra (psycopg 3); bounded connection pool with an operator-tunable ceiling; advisory-lock cold-start schema init for multi-replica safety; full DSN credential redaction; `cost_usd`/`latency_ms` stored as `DOUBLE PRECISION` for cross-backend value parity with the SQLite/filesystem backends. Establishes the Postgres adapter pattern the remaining five backends will copy ([258](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/258)).
- `atomic-agents serve` CLI subcommand — thin HTTP wrapper exposing `agent.call()` over four routes (`POST /agents/<name>/call`, `GET /agents/<name>/healthz`, `GET /agents/<name>/doctor`, `GET /agents`) for Cloud Run, GKE, Fly.io, Render, and any containerized environment ([342](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/342)).
- `atomic-agents-stack[serve]` optional extra (`starlette` + `uvicorn`). Base-package import pays zero cost — Starlette is never imported unless the extra is installed. (MUST 1.)
- Single-agent mode (`atomic-agents serve <name>`) scopes all four routes to the named agent; other agents return HTTP 404. `--all` mode serves every agent in the vault. Exactly one of `<agent>` or `--all` is required; providing both (or neither) exits with a clear error. (spec/37 §"CLI".)
- `trigger='http'` added to `_PRIMITIVE_BY_TRIGGER` → maps to `primitive='agent_call'` per spec/22. (MUST + spec/22.)
- `caller_identity` kwarg on `agent.call()` — serve layer passes the raw identity header value (e.g. `X-Goog-IAP-JWT-Assertion`); written into JSONL run records as `http_caller`. Field is present on **all** HTTP-triggered audit records including `lock_busy` and cost-skip paths. (MUST 7.)
- `run_id` reset moved to before lock acquisition in `agent.call()` so even refused-path (`lock_busy`, cost-skip) audit records carry a unique `run_id` per invocation. (MUST 8.)
- `critical=True` hard-refused via HTTP: serve layer hard-codes `critical=False`; a request body with `critical: true` returns HTTP 422 with a clear message. (MUST 5.)
- `max_tokens` / `temperature` / `model_override` input validation before dispatch: non-numeric `max_tokens`/`temperature` and non-string `model_override` return HTTP 422 (client error), not HTTP 500. Temperature range capped at `[0.0, 1.0]` (Anthropic's max; prevents in-range values above 1.0 from passing 422 validation but then 500-ing on Anthropic backends). A whitespace-only `work_item` (`" "`) also returns HTTP 422 — the truthiness guard is now `.strip()`-based so blank-but-whitespace prompts do not reach the LLM.
- `GET /agents/<name>/healthz` check 3 now requires `model.md` to be **present** before parsing — an absent `model.md` returns `503 degraded` rather than `200 ok`. Trade-off: agents that rely on default model config without a `model.md` will report degraded on `/healthz` until a `model.md` is added. The `atomic-agents init` path writes `model.md`; hand-rolled agents are the affected case.
- `make_app()` is self-contained: sets `app.state.identity_header` on construction so the returned app works without additional wiring from `_server.py`. Accepts `agent_name` and `identity_header` parameters.
- `is_loopback()` now uses `ipaddress.ip_address().is_loopback` to recognise the full `127.0.0.0/8` range (not just `127.0.0.1`) plus `localhost` and `::1`. (spec/37 §"No-auth default".)
- `serve.md` malformed port raises `ValueError` at parse time rather than silently falling back to default 8000. (MUST 2.) Same guard now wraps `--all` mode env-var parsing so a malformed `ATOMIC_AGENTS_SERVE_PORT` exits with a clean error on both CLI modes.
- `run_id` now included in HTTP 503 (lock_busy) and HTTP 402 (cost-skip) response bodies — callers can correlate the refused-call HTTP response to its JSONL audit record without joining against perimeter logs. CLAUDE.md principle 5.
- Route converters changed from `{name:path}` (matches slashes) to `{name:str}` (single non-slash segment) so multi-segment paths like `realagent/subdir` are rejected at the router rather than being admitted and relying solely on `safe_resolve_under` for containment. `safe_resolve_under` remains as defense-in-depth.
- `_generate_run_id()` now appends a `uuid4` hex fragment (`run-YYYYMMDD-HHMMSS-ffffff-xxxxxxxx`) to guarantee uniqueness under concurrent HTTP load where two requests could otherwise share the same microsecond timestamp. MUST 8.
- `AtomicAgentsError` 404 mapping narrowed: only errors whose message starts with `"Agent folder not found"` map to HTTP 404. Previously the broad `"not found"` substring check mislabelled server-misconfiguration errors (PersonaNotFound, rubric errors, etc.) as 404, corrupting retry semantics.
- DRAFT spec/37 — `atomic-agents serve` specification (10 MUSTs). Implementer Contract, no-auth default, CLI mode semantics, audit record shape, concurrency contract, MUST 9 executor pattern corrected to describe the shipped module-level ThreadPoolExecutor.
- `tests/test_serve_app.py` — Starlette `TestClient`-based conformance tests covering all four routes, single-agent scoping, MUST 5 (critical refused), MUST 7 (identity header passthrough), MUST 8 (run_id uniqueness), MUST 10 (path traversal 400), healthz degraded on missing model.md, 422 on bad numeric fields.
- `tests/test_serve_agent_layer.py` — agent-layer conformance tests for `http` trigger primitive mapping, `http_caller` in audit records (success path, None-omission, lock_busy path, cost-skip path), run_id uniqueness across sequential calls, and lock timeout values.

Fixed

- **`extras/gcp/` v0 topology fix — stateless Cloud Run + Compute Engine VM reference** ([395](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/395)). The original PR 1 blueprint ([#339](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/339)) included `gce-persistent-disk` volume blocks in `cloudrun-service.yaml` that do not exist in the Cloud Run v2 Volume schema (verified: Cloud Run v2 supports only `secret`, `cloudSqlInstance`, `emptyDir`, `nfs`, `gcs`). Even the supported NFS and GCS FUSE volume types are incompatible with the framework's `atomic_write` primitive (`tempfile.mkstemp` + `os.replace()` requires same-filesystem POSIX `rename(2)`; see TENSIONS.md T4). The fix: strips the unmountable `gce-persistent-disk` volume and per-subdir `volumeMounts` from `cloudrun-service.yaml` (now an honest stateless manifest with per-surface ephemeral annotations); adds `extras/gcp/compute-vm/` as the v0 stateful-today alternative (GCE VM + persistent ext4/xfs disk satisfying POSIX `rename(2)`); rewrites `extras/gcp/README.md` with the two-topology diagram, per-layer state mapping for both shapes, external-claim verification status table (all GCP platform claims cited to authoritative provider docs), and SIGTERM grace / upgrade runbook sections; absorbs security findings for aggregate spend control (billing budget alert section), Dockerfile non-root user (UID 10001), body-size Cloud Armor rule, and per-file not-yet-dogfooded assurance headers; re-grounds monitoring claims that were premised on the defunct persistent disk. README §Deployment shapes updated to reflect the two-topology split.

1.0.0

Added

This PR completes the 5-PR MCPServerRegistryBackend arc (201) and cuts v1.0.0. The arc ship streak is now 12 consecutive clean ships across 9 arcs. The v1.0 Protocol surface is closed: all 12 backend protocols (MemoryBackend, LLMBackend, JudgeBackend, LockBackend, LogBackend, AgentProfileBackend, ToolRegistryBackend, MandateBackend, PolicyBackend, PersonaBackend, CorpusBackend, MCPServerRegistryBackend) have locked specs + reference implementations + parametrized conformance suites. The framework is now `pip install atomic-agents-stack` and stable per SemVer Major.

- **MCPServerRegistryBackend HTTP write paths: install + uninstall** ([201](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/201) -- MCPServerRegistryBackend arc **PR 5 of 5**, closes the arc; spec/36 LOCKED). `HTTPMCPServerRegistryBackend.install(spec)` sends `POST /mcp-servers?agent_scope=<scope>` with the spec serialized via `spec.to_dict()` (env values must be unresolved `$VAR` form -- the method logs a WARNING if any env value lacks a `$` prefix). Returns `MCPServerRef` constructed by projecting from the input spec (not by parsing the 201 body per D-PR5-6); `source` is set to `f"{self._catalog_url}/mcp-servers/{spec.name}"`. `HTTPMCPServerRegistryBackend.uninstall(name)` sends `DELETE /mcp-servers/<name>?agent_scope=<scope>`; returns `None` on 204 (no `resp.json()` call per D-PR5-7); idempotent (204 on absent name is a no-op per MUST 9). Both methods follow the `_ensure_probed() -> check capability -> make call` ordering (D-PR5-1): capability gate blocks write calls on tier-1 probed backends without making a network request.

- **405 mid-session tier regression handler** ([201](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/201) PR 5). When `POST /mcp-servers` or `DELETE /mcp-servers/<name>` returns HTTP 405 (catalog demoted to read-only mid-session), the `_handle_tier_regression(operation)` helper re-probes via `refresh_capabilities()`, updates `_cached_capabilities` inside `_capabilities_lock`, and raises `NotImplementedError` with an operator-readable tier-change message naming the previous/new tier and the affected operation. Uses `_safe_catalog_url` in all error messages per MUST 4 (D-PR5-9). If the re-probe itself fails, raises `MCPRegistryUnavailable` with "Capability cache may be stale" (D-PR5-4). If re-probe returns tier-2 despite the 405 (inconsistent server), trusts the 405 and raises `NotImplementedError` with "inconsistent server" message (D-PR5-3, B-F3). No silent retry.

- **409 collision handling for install** ([201](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/201) PR 5). `POST /mcp-servers` returning HTTP 409 raises `MCPServerAlreadyInstalled` (not `MCPRegistryUnavailable`). Added `expect_409_means_collision=True` parameter to `_handle_http_error` following the existing `expect_404_means_not_found_for_name` pattern. `MCPServerAlreadyInstalled` added to `http.py` import block (A-F1 fix).

- **spec/36 LOCKED** at 201 PR 5. DRAFT marker removed; six pre-dispatch corrections applied (exception table rows for 409/405/204; HTTP install/uninstall semantics section; Decision 5 PR 5 column wording correction; test count corrections; inline TODO removal; inconsistent-server edge case clarification). HTTP install/uninstall semantics section documents POST/DELETE wire protocol, error mapping, and client-constructs-MCPServerRef discipline.

- **Conformance suite MockTransport upgraded for tier-2 write paths** ([201](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/201) PR 5, D-PR5-9). `_default_mock_transport()` in `tests/test_mcp_server_registry_conformance.py` now returns a tier-2 capability response, serves `Allow: GET, POST, DELETE` on OPTIONS, handles `POST /mcp-servers` (201 on first install, 409 on duplicate name via closure-scoped dict per D-PR5-10), and handles `DELETE /mcp-servers/<name>` (204, idempotent). MUST 3 `test_capability_honesty_install` and `test_capability_honesty_uninstall` tests now call `list_mcp_servers()` before reading `caps` to trigger the HTTP probe, so the True-branch actually runs against HTTP (C-F1 fix). MUST 4 parametrized tests added for URL credential redaction in install/uninstall error paths (D-PR5-11).

- **12 new HTTP backend write-path tests** in `tests/test_mcp_server_registry_http_backend.py` (section j). Covers: POST 201 happy path, POST 409 collision, POST 405 tier regression (three sub-cases: tier-1 re-probe, re-probe failure, inconsistent tier-2 re-probe), DELETE 204 happy path, DELETE absent-name idempotency, DELETE 405 tier regression, capability gate (no POST on tier-1 probed backend), env-literal WARNING, auth header on POST and DELETE, MUST 4 credential redaction in MCPServerAlreadyInstalled message. Plus regression guard: `test_capabilities_before_first_probe_still_conservative_after_pr5` asserts pre-probe conservative default remains False/False at PR 5 (C-F7 guard per B-F11 and Decision 6).

- **v1.0.0 release tooling**. `pyproject.toml`: version bumped `0.13.0` to `1.0.0`; classifier flipped from `Development Status :: 3 - Alpha` to `Development Status :: 5 - Production/Stable`; `twine>=6.0` added to `[dependency-groups].dev`; `[tool.hatch.build.targets.sdist]` exclusion config added (`tests/`, `docs/`, `extras/`, `.claude/`, `.github/`). `atomic_agents/__init__.py`: `__version__` bumped to `1.0.0`. `.github/workflows/publish-to-pypi.yml` added (triggered on `v1.*` tag push; runs `uv build` + `twine check` + `uv publish` via `UV_PUBLISH_TOKEN` secret; first publish is manual).

- **README rewritten for PyPI rendering**. All relative links to `docs/`, `extras/`, `samples/`, images, and root-level files rewritten to absolute `https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/blob/main/...` GitHub URLs. Hero image `<picture>` block updated to raw.githubusercontent.com URLs. Quick Start section adds `pip install atomic-agents-stack` / `uv add atomic-agents-stack` as the preferred install path; clone path stays for contributors. Version badge updated to 1.0.0 stable. Backend table MCPServerRegistryBackend row flipped from "In progress (PR 4 of 5)" to "Shipped". Status block updated: "Twelve of twelve backend protocols shipped", "v1.0.0, stable".

- **CLAUDE.md 12th backend lock-paragraph** for MCPServerRegistryBackend (mirrors CorpusBackend lock-paragraph shape). Covers Protocol surface (list/load/load_all/validate/install/uninstall/capabilities/refresh_capabilities/close), key decisions D1-D9, conformance suite coverage, capability flag evolution table, tier negotiation, and the cliff this closes (v1.0 Protocol surface -- operators with a managed MCP catalog or private HTTP catalog registry can install/uninstall MCP servers from the same `agent.call()` flow as home-user filesystem operators). Architecture diagram MCPServerRegistry entry flipped from yellow to green (locked at 201 PR 5). Where-things-live spec count updated to "32 locked + 3 drafts". Status block updated to v1.0.0, stable, "Twelve backend protocols shipped."

All five PR squash hashes listed (PR 5 as placeholder pending merge). "Eleven backend protocols shipped" copy updated to "Twelve backend protocols shipped." v1.0 close acknowledged.

- **docs/deployment/release-runbook.md** additions: Pre-publish smoke section (5-command sequence: `uv build` + `twine check dist/*` + clean-venv install + `atomic-agents --version` + `atomic-agents doctor` + version assertion); TestPyPI smoke section (publish to test.pypi.org first, verify rendering, install from TestPyPI, smoke, then production PyPI); Rollback contract section (yank semantics -- PyPI `--yank` marks as not recommended for new installs without removing; v1.0.1 is the correct recovery path, not delete-and-republish).

- **HTTPMCPServerRegistryBackend read paths + tier negotiation + httpx exception mapping + conformance parametrize** ([201](https://github.com/dep0we/atomic-agents-stack/issues/201) -- MCPServerRegistryBackend arc **PR 4 of 5**). `atomic_agents/mcp_registry/http.py` ships `HTTPMCPServerRegistryBackend` with the full Decision 4 five-step capability probe sequence (`GET /capabilities` 200 parses tier from body; 404 falls through to `OPTIONS /mcp-servers` Allow-header set-membership inference; OPTIONS 404 falls back to conservative tier 1; 5xx raises `MCPRegistryUnavailable`; 401 raises `MCPRegistryAuthRequired`; other non-404 4xx raises `MCPRegistryUnavailable` per B-F8 -- no silent tier-1 fallback). `make_http_mcp_server_registry_backend_from_url(url)` factory reads `ATOMIC_AGENTS_MCP_SERVER_REGISTRY_AUTH_TOKEN` from the environment. The `[http]` extra (`httpx>=0.27`) is opt-in; filesystem operators pay zero import cost (lazy import inside the http branch). `MCPServerSpec.to_dict()` and `MCPServerSpec.from_dict()` promoted to public methods on the class; `profile/types.py` helpers delegate. `get_default_mcp_server_registry_backend` gains the `http` branch reading `ATOMIC_AGENTS_MCP_SERVER_REGISTRY_BACKEND_URL` + `ATOMIC_AGENTS_MCP_SERVER_REGISTRY_AUTH_TOKEN`. httpx exception mapping covers the full public tree: `LocalProtocolError` and `DecodingError` map to `MCPRegistryDescriptorInvalid`; all timeout variants, network errors, and remote protocol errors map to `MCPRegistryUnavailable`; `httpx.InvalidURL` (does not inherit from `HTTPError`) maps to `ValueError` via a separate except clause; `httpx.HTTPError` is the final catch-all for any future subclass. Capability probe failure cache (`probe_failure_cache_s=60.0`) suppresses re-probes within the window; `refresh_capabilities()` bypasses the cache. Threading: `threading.Lock` guards only the cache-check decision and cache-write assignment; the HTTP probe runs outside the lock so concurrent first-call callers do not serialize against network latency. `MCPServerRef.source` uses the raw `catalog_url` (per spec/36 line 228: `source=f"{catalog_url}/mcp-servers/{name}"` is the canonical wire contract). The recommended operator pattern for credentials is the `ATOMIC_AGENTS_MCP_SERVER_REGISTRY_AUTH_TOKEN` env var rather than embedding credentials in `catalog_url`; the factory's `ValueError` redacts credentials when an unsupported scheme is passed so a paste mistake on the env var does not leak. `auth_token` never appears in any error message, log line, or `__repr__`. Response body validation rejects malformed JSON, missing `servers` key, missing required spec fields (`name`, `command`), and MUST 1 charset violations in returned server names. `load_all_mcp_servers()` uses a single `GET /mcp-servers?expand=spec` bulk call instead of N+1 per-name requests (MUST 10). spec/36 PR 4 amendments ship inline: new subsections for HTTP wire format, tier negotiation, capability handshake (static-vs-runtime view), per-scope filtering (catalog MUST filter server-side; org-wide returns are non-conformant), exception surface (httpx mapping table with `httpx.InvalidURL` separate catch noted), and default factory (new env vars). **Test delta: +75 net new (3232 before PR 4, 3307 after)**. `tests/test_mcp_server_registry_http_backend.py` NEW (54 tests using `httpx.MockTransport` -- zero new dev dependency -- covering: MUST 2 side-effect-free construction including lazy-httpx-import guard; all 5 Decision 4 probe branches plus 401, non-404 4xx, and reordered Allow header; full httpx exception mapping including `LocalProtocolError` to `DescriptorInvalid`, `DecodingError` to `DescriptorInvalid` (deterministic via MockTransport injection), `InvalidURL` to `ValueError` via injection, `HTTPError` catch-all, and `RuntimeError` from a closed `httpx.Client` to `MCPRegistryUnavailable` (closes the Adv-F2 race); 5 response body validation defense-in-depth tests; MUST 10 bulk endpoint tests including full-field equality with non-default `args`/`env`/`description` and env-var resolution; 4 auth and URL credential redaction tests; 3 capability lifecycle tests; review-army follow-up tests covering `MCPServerSpec.to_dict/from_dict` public round-trip + extra-key forward-compat + required-key `KeyError`, OPTIONS probe 5xx and 401 handling per Adv-F3, concurrent first-call probe verification per D-PR4-3, `agent_scope` query-param forwarding verification, success-cache verification, factory function tests including credential-redacting `ValueError` and env-var auth-token read, `catalog_url` query-string normalization per Adv-F4, and `MCPServerRef.source` using the raw `catalog_url` per spec/36). `tests/test_mcp_server_registry_conformance.py` flips `params=["filesystem"]` to `params=["filesystem", "http"]` on both `backend_factory` and `populated_backend` fixtures;
